const ic = k => `<svg class="ic" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">${IC[k]}</svg>`;
function call(fn, ...args) {
  return new Promise((res, rej) => {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(res)
      .withFailureHandler(e => rej(new Error(String((e && e.message) || e).replace(/^(Exception|Error):\s*/, ''))))[fn](...args);
  });
}
let toastT;
function toast(msg) { const t = $('#toast'); t.textContent = msg; t.hidden = false; clearTimeout(toastT); toastT = setTimeout(() => { t.hidden = true; }, 3200); }
function modal(html, center) { S.onClose = null; const m = $('#modal'); m.innerHTML = `<div class="sheet${center ? ' center' : ''}" role="dialog" aria-modal="true">${html}</div>`; m.hidden = false; hydrate(m); const f = m.querySelector('[autofocus],button'); if (f) f.focus(); }
function closeModal() { $('#modal').hidden = true; $('#modal').innerHTML = ''; const f = S.onClose; S.onClose = null; if (f) f(); }
function store(k, v) { try { if (v === undefined) return localStorage.getItem(k); localStorage.setItem(k, v); } catch (e) { return null; } }

/* ===== Hall Pass Kiosk adapter (before ASL Study's seating code) =====
   ASL Study's seating chart, group maker and name picker run inside its app frame: VIEW (screens),
   FORM (forms), go() (show a screen), shell() and teachNav() (its menus). This page has only these
   three screens, so the frame is small. Rules from ASL Study: no slash-star or double-slash
   comments inside template strings or regexes, and no closing script tag in strings. */
const VIEW = {};
const FORM = {};
let kioskLink = '';      /* this app's web address (back to the teacher view) */

function hydrate(root) { if (typeof evenGrids === 'function') evenGrids(root); }
function shell() {}

function go(v) {
  S.view = v;
  view.innerHTML = VIEW[v]();
  hydrate(view);
  window.scrollTo(0, 0);
}

/* the menu under each screen's title: the three tools, saved layouts, and back to the teacher view */
const teachNav = cur => {
  const b = (v, label) => `<button class="btn${cur === v ? ' sun' : ' ghost'}" data-a="kgo" data-v="${v}">${label}</button>`;
  return `<div class="knav noprint">${b('seats', '🪑 Seating chart')}${b('groups', '👥 Group maker')}${b('picker', '🎲 Name picker')}` +
    (cur === 'seats' ? '<button class="btn ghost" data-a="ksavelayout">💾 Save as a layout</button><button class="btn ghost" data-a="klayouts">📂 Saved layouts</button>' : '') +
    (kioskLink ? `<a class="btn ghost" href="${esc(kioskLink)}?view=teacher" target="_top">← Teacher view</a>` : '') + '</div>';
};

function evenGrids(root) { root.querySelectorAll('.stats, .grid, .chargrid, .bgrid').forEach(g => { const c = g.childElementCount; if (c < 2 || g.closest('#modal')) return; g.classList.add('even'); g.style.setProperty('--cn', gridCols(c, [2, 3])); g.style.setProperty('--cw', gridCols(c, [4, 3, 5, 2])); if (!g.closest('.home, .me2')) g.style.setProperty('--cx', [6, 5, 4].find(k => c % k === 0) || gridCols(c, [4, 3, 5, 2])); }); }

/* ───────── seating chart drawings ─────────
   Top-down floor-plan pictures, drawn to fill a piece of w by h squares (100 units a square). Doors, windows, the board and the screen sit on the
   nearest wall (side: top, bottom, left or right). Touching squares of the same thing are joined into one piece before drawing. */
const SVG_INK = '#3D3328';
function stWall(side, W, H, draw) {      /* draw(L, D) pictures a wall along the bottom (y = D); this turns it to the right side */
  if (side === 'top') return '<g transform="rotate(180 ' + W / 2 + ' ' + H / 2 + ')">' + draw(W, H) + '</g>';
  if (side === 'left') return '<g transform="matrix(0,1,-1,0,' + W + ',0)">' + draw(H, W) + '</g>';
  if (side === 'right') return '<g transform="matrix(0,-1,1,0,0,' + H + ')">' + draw(H, W) + '</g>';
  return draw(W, H);
}
const stLine = (x1, y1, x2, y2, w, col, extra) => '<line x1="' + x1 + '" y1="' + y1 + '" x2="' + x2 + '" y2="' + y2 + '" stroke="' + (col || SVG_INK) + '" stroke-width="' + w + '" stroke-linecap="round"' + (extra || '') + '/>';
const stRect = (x, y, w, h, rx, fill, sw, stroke) => '<rect x="' + x + '" y="' + y + '" width="' + Math.max(0, w) + '" height="' + Math.max(0, h) + '" rx="' + rx + '" fill="' + fill + '" stroke="' + (stroke || SVG_INK) + '" stroke-width="' + (sw == null ? 4 : sw) + '"/>';
const stCirc = (x, y, r, fill, sw) => '<circle cx="' + x + '" cy="' + y + '" r="' + r + '" fill="' + fill + '" stroke="' + SVG_INK + '" stroke-width="' + (sw == null ? 4 : sw) + '"/>';
const ST_DRAW = {
  /* opt.h: the hinge on the left or right as you face the door from inside the room; opt.s: swings in (into the room) or out */
  door: (W, H, side, opt) => stWall(side, W, H, (L, D) => {
    const o = opt || {}, out = o.s === 'out', y = D - 6, two = L > 150, r = Math.min(two ? (L - 24) / 2 : L - 24, out ? 60 : D - 16);
    let s = stLine(0, y, 10, y, 12) + stLine(L - 10, y, L, y, 12);
    const leaf = (hx, dir) => { const ty = out ? y + r : y - r, ex = hx + dir * r, sweep = out ? (dir > 0 ? 0 : 1) : (dir > 0 ? 1 : 0); return stLine(hx, y, hx, ty, 6, '#8A5A2B') + stCirc(hx, y, 5, '#3D3328', 0) + '<path d="M' + hx + ' ' + ty + ' A' + r + ' ' + r + ' 0 0 ' + sweep + ' ' + ex + ' ' + y + '" fill="none" stroke="#8A5A2B" stroke-width="3" stroke-dasharray="8 6"/>'; };
    return s + (two ? leaf(12, 1) + leaf(L - 12, -1) : o.h === 'left' ? leaf(L - 12, -1) : leaf(12, 1));
  }),
  window: (W, H, side) => stWall(side, W, H, (L, D) => {
    let s = stRect(2, D - 22, L - 4, 18, 3, '#CFE9F8', 4) + stLine(6, D - 13, L - 6, D - 13, 2, '#7FB3D5');
    for (let x = 100; x < L - 20; x += 100) s += stLine(x, D - 22, x, D - 4, 3);
    return s;
  }),
  board: (W, H, side) => stWall(side, W, H, (L, D) => stRect(8, D - 26, L - 16, 20, 3, '#2E4B3C', 4) + stLine(14, D - 4, L - 14, D - 4, 4, '#9A7B55')),
  screen: (W, H, side) => stWall(side, W, H, (L, D) => stRect(10, D - 16, L - 20, 10, 3, '#F4F5F8', 4) + stLine(10, D - 30, L - 10, D - 30, 2, '#9AA3B2', ' stroke-dasharray="6 6"')),
  teacher: (W, H) => stRect(10, 30, W - 20, H - 44, 8, '#C89B6A', 4) + stRect(W / 2 - 24, 6, 48, 24, 10, '#7D8FAD', 4) + stRect(W / 2 - 22, 46, 44, 26, 3, '#E9ECF2', 3) + stCirc(W - 34, H - 34, 8, '#D94F4F', 3),
  shelf: (W, H) => { let s = stRect(8, 8, W - 16, H - 16, 4, '#8E6038', 4); const cols = ['#C94F4F', '#4C6FE0', '#E8B33E', '#3E9B6C', '#8B5FBF']; const horiz = W >= H; for (let i = 0, p = 18; p < (horiz ? W : H) - 22; i++, p += 14) s += horiz ? stRect(p, 16, 10, H - 32, 2, cols[i % 5], 0) : stRect(16, p, W - 32, 10, 2, cols[i % 5], 0); return s; },
  cabinet: (W, H) => { let s = stRect(10, 10, W - 20, H - 20, 5, '#B9C0CC', 4); for (let x = 50; x < W - 20; x += 50) s += stLine(x, 14, x, H - 14, 2); for (let x = 50; x < W - 20; x += 100) s += stCirc(x - 8, H / 2, 3, SVG_INK, 0) + stCirc(x + 8, H / 2, 3, SVG_INK, 0); return s; },
  sink: (W, H) => { let s = stRect(10, 14, W - 20, H - 28, 8, '#DFE5EC', 4); for (let x = 50; x < W; x += 100) s += '<ellipse cx="' + x + '" cy="' + (H / 2 + 6) + '" rx="26" ry="20" fill="#F7FAFC" stroke="' + SVG_INK + '" stroke-width="3"/>' + stCirc(x, H / 2 - 22, 6, '#9AA3B2', 3); return s; },
  printer: (W, H) => stRect(18, 26, W - 36, H - 44, 6, '#D5DAE2', 4) + stRect(W / 2 - 20, 12, 40, 18, 2, '#FFFFFF', 3) + stLine(W / 2 - 22, H - 32, W / 2 + 22, H - 32, 4),
  trash: (W, H) => {      /* a green trash bin with crumpled paper, and a blue recycling bin with the three chasing arrows */
    const y = H / 2, l = W / 2 - 23, rr = W / 2 + 23, R = 11;
    let s = stRect(l - 21, y - 25, 42, 50, 12, '#86B08F', 4) + stRect(l - 14, y - 18, 28, 36, 8, '#5C8B69', 2.5);
    const ball = (x, y2, r) => stCirc(x, y2, r, '#FBF6EA', 2.5) + '<path d="M' + (x - r * .55) + ' ' + (y2 + 1) + ' q' + (r * .3) + ' ' + (-r * .6) + ' ' + (r * .6) + ' 0 t' + (r * .5) + ' 0" fill="none" stroke="#B9AE95" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linecap="round"/>';
    s += ball(l - 4, y - 5, 8) + ball(l + 5, y + 8, 6.5);
    s += stRect(rr - 21, y - 25, 42, 50, 12, '#5B9BE0', 4) + stRect(rr - 14, y - 18, 28, 36, 8, '#3F7FC4', 2.5);
    const v = [-90, 30, 150].map(d => [rr + R * Math.cos(d * Math.PI / 180), y + 2 + R * Math.sin(d * Math.PI / 180)]);
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) { const p = v[i], q = v[(i + 1) % 3], ax = p[0] + (q[0] - p[0]) * .18, ay = p[1] + (q[1] - p[1]) * .18, bx = p[0] + (q[0] - p[0]) * .78, by = p[1] + (q[1] - p[1]) * .78, ang = Math.atan2(q[1] - p[1], q[0] - p[0]);
      const h1 = [bx - 6 * Math.cos(ang - .6), by - 6 * Math.sin(ang - .6)], h2 = [bx - 6 * Math.cos(ang + .6), by - 6 * Math.sin(ang + .6)];
      s += '<path d="M' + ax.toFixed(1) + ' ' + ay.toFixed(1) + ' L' + bx.toFixed(1) + ' ' + by.toFixed(1) + ' M' + h1[0].toFixed(1) + ' ' + h1[1].toFixed(1) + ' L' + bx.toFixed(1) + ' ' + by.toFixed(1) + ' L' + h2[0].toFixed(1) + ' ' + h2[1].toFixed(1) + '" fill="none" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="3.2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>'; }
    return s;
  },
  plant: (W, H) => { const cx = W / 2, cy = H / 2; let s = stCirc(cx, cy, 22, '#A86E44', 4); for (let a = 0; a < 6; a++) { const t = a * Math.PI / 3, x = cx + Math.cos(t) * 22, y = cy + Math.sin(t) * 22; s += '<ellipse cx="' + x.toFixed(1) + '" cy="' + y.toFixed(1) + '" rx="17" ry="9" transform="rotate(' + (a * 60) + ' ' + x.toFixed(1) + ' ' + y.toFixed(1) + ')" fill="#4E9B5B" stroke="' + SVG_INK + '" stroke-width="3"/>'; } return s + stCirc(cx, cy, 9, '#67B26F', 3); },
  rug: (W, H) => { let s = stRect(12, 12, W - 24, H - 24, 14, '#E2A68E', 4) + stRect(24, 24, W - 48, H - 48, 8, 'none', 3, '#B5694F'); for (let y = 18; y < H - 14; y += 10) s += stLine(4, y, 12, y, 2, '#B5694F') + stLine(W - 12, y, W - 4, y, 2, '#B5694F'); return s; },
  supplies: (W, H) => stRect(16, 22, W - 32, H - 40, 5, '#F2D388', 4) + [0, 1, 2, 3].map(i => stLine(28 + i * 14, 30, 28 + i * 14, H - 28, 6, ['#D94F4F', '#4C6FE0', '#3E9B6C', '#8B5FBF'][i])).join(''),
  aisle: (W, H) => stLine(W >= H ? 14 : W / 2, W >= H ? H / 2 : 14, W >= H ? W - 14 : W / 2, W >= H ? H / 2 : H - 14, 4, '#B9BFCC', ' stroke-dasharray="4 14"'),
  /* seats: drawn for students facing the front (up) */
  desk: (W, H) => stRect(10, 6, W - 20, 36, 5, '#D8B888', 4) + stRect(W / 2 - 24, 52, 48, 36, 12, '#8FA2C2', 4),
  worktable: (W, H) => { let s = stRect(8, 8, W - 16, H - 16, 10, '#B9A58C', 4); for (let y = 30; y < H - 20; y += 26) s += stLine(22, y, W - 22, y, 1.5, '#9C8970'); return s; },
  table: (W, H) => {      /* a table with a chair at each seat: top and bottom rows on the outside, middle rows on the ends */
    const w = Math.max(1, Math.round(W / 100)), h = Math.max(1, Math.round(H / 95)), mid = h >= 3, L = mid ? 30 : 8, T = h >= 2 ? 30 : 8;
    let s = stRect(L, T, W - L - (mid ? 30 : 8), H - T - 30, 12, '#C99A64', 4);
    for (let y = T + 20; y < H - 40; y += 24) s += stLine(L + 12, y, W - (mid ? 42 : 20), y, 1.5, '#A87A48');
    for (let j = 0; j < h; j++) for (let i = 0; i < w; i++) {
      const side = h === 1 ? 'bottom' : j === 0 ? 'top' : j === h - 1 ? 'bottom' : i === 0 ? 'left' : i === w - 1 ? 'right' : '', x0 = i * 100, y0 = j * 95;
      if (side === 'top') s += stRect(x0 + 28, y0 + 3, 44, 22, 9, '#8FA2C2', 3.5); if (side === 'bottom') s += stRect(x0 + 28, y0 + 95 - 25, 44, 22, 9, '#8FA2C2', 3.5);
      if (side === 'left') s += stRect(3, y0 + 26, 22, 44, 9, '#8FA2C2', 3.5); if (side === 'right') s += stRect(W - 25, y0 + 26, 22, 44, 9, '#8FA2C2', 3.5);
    }
    return s;
  },
  lab: (W, H) => { let s = stRect(6, 6, W - 12, H * .55, 6, '#8FA6BE', 4) + stRect(12, 12, W - 24, 24, 3, '#2F3B4A', 0); for (let x = 50; x < W; x += 100) s += stCirc(x, 24, 8, '#C9D4E0', 3) + stCirc(x + 26, 24, 4, '#E8B33E', 2) + stRect(x - 22, H - 32, 44, 26, 10, '#8FA2C2', 3.5); return s; },
  comp: (W, H) => { let s = stRect(6, 6, W - 12, 44, 5, '#CDD3DD', 4); for (let x = 50; x < W; x += 100) s += stRect(x - 26, 12, 52, 10, 2, '#2B3445', 3) + stRect(x - 18, 30, 36, 12, 2, '#F4F5F8', 2) + stRect(x - 22, H - 34, 44, 26, 10, '#8FA2C2', 3.5); return s; }
};
/* the whole picture for one piece: w by h squares; flip turns seats around for the students' view */
function stSvg(type, w, h, side, flip, full, opt) {
  const W = w * 100, H = h * 95, f = ST_DRAW[type] || (() => '');
  let body = f(W, H, side || 'bottom', opt), ang = type === 'desk' ? stDeg(opt && opt.f) : 0;      /* which way a desk faces */
  if (flip && /^(desk|table|lab|comp|teacher)$/.test(type)) ang += 180;
  if (ang % 360) body = '<g transform="rotate(' + ang + ' ' + W / 2 + ' ' + H / 2 + ')">' + body + '</g>';
  return '<svg ' + (full ? 'xmlns="http:' + '/' + '/www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="' + full[0] + '" height="' + full[1] + '" ' : '') + 'viewBox="0 0 ' + W + ' ' + H + '" preserveAspectRatio="none" aria-hidden="true">' + body + '</svg>';
}

/* ───────── seating chart (teacher) ─────────
   Step 1, Room layout: seats (desk, table seat, lab station, computer seat) and room things on a grid; touching squares of the same thing join
   into one piece. Seat areas: ones the teacher paints and names, and ones worked out from the room (front rows, left side, near the teacher's desk...).
   Step 2, Seat students: rules (sit together, keep apart, sits in an area), Shuffle, swap, pin. Saved per class as it changes. Only the teacher sees it. */
const ST_LAYOUTS = [['rows', 'Rows'], ['pairs', 'Pairs'], ['groups', 'Groups of 4'], ['horse', 'Horseshoe'], ['arc', 'Semicircle'], ['empty', 'Empty room']];
function stLayout(kind) {
  const d = [], add = (r, c) => d.push(r + ',' + c);
  if (kind === 'empty') return { rows: 6, cols: 8, desks: d };
  if (kind === 'arc') { const cx = 5, seen = new Set(); [3.2, 5.4].forEach(R => { const n = Math.round(R * 2.4); for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) { const a = Math.PI * i / n, col = Math.round(cx + R * Math.cos(a)), row = Math.round(R * Math.sin(a) * .95); const k = row + ',' + col; if (!seen.has(k) && row < 7) { seen.add(k); d.push(k); } } });
    const c = { rows: 7, cols: 11, desks: d, face: {} }; d.forEach(k => { const a = stAim(c, k); if (a) c.face[k] = a; }); return c; }
  if (kind === 'pairs') { for (let r = 0; r < 6; r++) [0, 1, 3, 4, 6, 7].forEach(c => add(r, c)); return { rows: 6, cols: 8, desks: d }; }
  if (kind === 'groups') { for (const r0 of [0, 3, 6]) for (const c0 of [0, 3, 6]) { add(r0, c0); add(r0, c0 + 1); add(r0 + 1, c0); add(r0 + 1, c0 + 1); } return { rows: 8, cols: 8, desks: d }; }
  if (kind === 'horse') { for (let r = 0; r < 6; r++) { add(r, 0); add(r, 9); } for (let c = 1; c < 9; c++) add(5, c); for (let r = 1; r < 4; r++) { add(r, 2); add(r, 7); } for (let c = 3; c < 7; c++) add(3, c); return { rows: 6, cols: 10, desks: d }; }
  for (let r = 0; r < 5; r++) for (let c = 0; c < 6; c++) add(r, c);
  return { rows: 5, cols: 6, desks: d };
}
const stNew = () => Object.assign(stLayout('rows'), { seats: {}, pins: {}, rules: [], flip: false, items: {}, kinds: {}, zones: [] });
/* [key, name]. Seats: students sit there. Room things: nobody does. */
const ST_SEATS = [['desk', 'Desk'], ['table', 'Table seat'], ['lab', 'Lab station'], ['comp', 'Computer seat']];
const ST_THINGS = [['worktable', 'Work table'], ['door', 'Door'], ['window', 'Window'], ['board', 'Board'], ['screen', 'Screen'], ['teacher', 'Teacher’s desk'], ['shelf', 'Bookshelf'], ['cabinet', 'Cabinet'], ['sink', 'Sink'],
  ['printer', 'Printer'], ['trash', 'Trash and recycling'], ['plant', 'Plant'], ['rug', 'Rug'], ['supplies', 'Supplies'], ['aisle', 'Walkway']];
const ST_ALONE = ['desk', 'printer', 'trash', 'plant', 'supplies'];      /* these never join their neighbours */
const stThing = k => ST_THINGS.find(t => t[0] === k);
const stSeatKind = k => ST_SEATS.find(t => t[0] === k);
/* areas the app works out from the room: [key, name] */
const ST_AUTO = [['front', 'Front two rows'], ['back', 'Back two rows'], ['left', 'Left side'], ['right', 'Right side'], ['near-teacher', 'Near the teacher’s desk'], ['near-door', 'Near the door'], ['near-window', 'Near the windows'], ['near-board', 'Near the board']];
const ST_ZCOL = ['#E8A33D', '#3E9B6C', '#8B5FBF', '#D94F4F', '#2F8FC7', '#C2559A'];
const stName = e => { const s = S.st.data.students.find(x => x[0] === e); return s ? s[1] : ''; };
const stRC = k => k.split(',').map(Number);
const stNear = (a, b) => { const [r1, c1] = stRC(a), [r2, c2] = stRC(b); return a !== b && Math.abs(r1 - r2) <= 1 && Math.abs(c1 - c2) <= 1; };
const stSide = (a, b) => { const [r1, c1] = stRC(a), [r2, c2] = stRC(b); return r1 === r2 && Math.abs(c1 - c2) === 1; };
const stZoneName = (c, z) => { const a = ST_AUTO.find(x => x[0] === z); if (a) return a[1]; const m = (c.zones || []).find(x => x.id === z); return m ? m.name : 'an area that was removed'; };
/* the seats in an area */
function stZone(c, z) {
  const m = (c.zones || []).find(x => x.id === z); if (m) return new Set(m.cells.filter(k => c.desks.indexOf(k) >= 0));
  const seats = c.desks.map(k => [k].concat(stRC(k))), rows = [...new Set(seats.map(s => s[1]))].sort((x, y) => x - y), mid = (c.cols - 1) / 2;
  const nearTo = type => { const at = Object.keys(c.items || {}).filter(k => c.items[k] === type || (type === 'board' && c.items[k] === 'screen')); return new Set(seats.filter(s => at.some(t => { const [r, col] = stRC(t); return Math.max(Math.abs(r - s[1]), Math.abs(col - s[2])) <= 2; })).map(s => s[0])); };
  if (z === 'front') return new Set(seats.filter(s => rows.indexOf(s[1]) < 2).map(s => s[0]));
  if (z === 'back') return new Set(seats.filter(s => rows.indexOf(s[1]) >= rows.length - 2).map(s => s[0]));
  if (z === 'left') return new Set(seats.filter(s => s[2] < mid).map(s => s[0]));
  if (z === 'right') return new Set(seats.filter(s => s[2] > mid).map(s => s[0]));
  if (/^near-/.test(z)) return nearTo(z.slice(5));
  return new Set();
}
VIEW.seats = () => {
  S.st = S.st || { cls: '', mode: 'seat', pick: '', tool: 'desk' };
  stLoad(S.st.cls);
  return `<div class="stack"><div class="row"><div class="grow"><p class="eyebrow">Teacher</p><h1>Seating Chart</h1></div></div>${teachNav('seats')}<div id="stbody"><div class="splash"><div class="spin"></div></div></div></div>`;
};
function stLoad(cls) {
  call('api_seating', AUTH, cls).then(d => {
    S.st.data = d; S.st.cls = d.cls; S.st.pick = '';
    const c = d.chart || stNew(), have = new Set(d.students.map(s => s[0]));
    ['desks', 'rules', 'zones'].forEach(k => { if (!Array.isArray(c[k])) c[k] = []; }); c.seats = c.seats || {}; c.pins = c.pins || {}; c.items = c.items || {}; c.kinds = c.kinds || {}; c.doors = c.doors || {}; c.face = c.face || {}; c.walls = c.walls || {}; if (!Array.isArray(c.off)) c.off = [];
    if (Array.isArray(c.pref)) { if (c.pref.length && !c.zones.some(z => z.id === 'pref')) c.zones.unshift({ id: 'pref', name: 'Preferred seats', color: ST_ZCOL[0], cells: c.pref }); delete c.pref; }      /* charts from before seat areas */
    c.rules.forEach(r => { if (r.t === 'pref') { r.t = 'zone'; r.z = 'pref'; } });
    Object.keys(c.seats).forEach(k => { if (!have.has(c.seats[k]) || c.desks.indexOf(k) < 0) delete c.seats[k]; });      /* students who left, seats that were removed */
    Object.keys(c.pins).forEach(e => { if (!have.has(e) || c.seats[c.pins[e]] !== e) delete c.pins[e]; });
    c.rules = c.rules.filter(r => have.has(r.a) && (r.t === 'zone' || have.has(r.b)));
    if (!d.chart) S.st.mode = 'desks';      /* a class with no chart yet starts by setting up the room */
    S.st.chart = c; if (S.view === 'seats') { stPaint(); let seen = ''; try { seen = localStorage.getItem('sh-st-help') || ''; } catch (e) {} if (!seen) { try { localStorage.setItem('sh-st-help', '1'); } catch (e) {} ACT.sthelp(); } }
  }).catch(e => { const b = $('#stbody'); if (b) b.innerHTML = '<p>' + esc(e.message) + '</p>'; });
}
function stSave() {
  clearTimeout(S.st.saveT); const cls = S.st.cls, chart = S.st.chart; stNote('Saving…');
  S.st.saveT = setTimeout(() => call('api_seatingSave', AUTH, cls, chart).then(() => stNote('Saved')).catch(e => stNote(e.message)), 700);
}
const stNote = t => { const el = $('#stsaved'); if (el) el.textContent = t; };
/* joins touching squares of the same thing into rectangles: [type, row, col, height, width] in room squares */
function stPieces(c) {
  const type = k => (c.items || {})[k] || (c.desks.indexOf(k) >= 0 ? ((c.kinds || {})[k] || 'desk') : ''), used = new Set(), out = [];
  for (let r = 0; r < c.rows; r++) for (let col = 0; col < c.cols; col++) {
    const k = r + ',' + col, t = type(k); if (!t || used.has(k)) continue;
    let w = 1, h = 1;
    if (ST_ALONE.indexOf(t) < 0) {
      while (col + w < c.cols && type(r + ',' + (col + w)) === t && !used.has(r + ',' + (col + w))) w++;
      const rowOk = rr => { for (let x = col; x < col + w; x++) { const kk = rr + ',' + x; if (type(kk) !== t || used.has(kk)) return false; } return true; };
      while (r + h < c.rows && rowOk(r + h)) h++;
    }
    for (let y = r; y < r + h; y++) for (let x = col; x < col + w; x++) used.add(y + ',' + x);
    out.push([t, r, col, h, w]);
  }
  return out;
}
/* where a piece sits on screen (flip turns the room around) and which wall it is nearest */
const ST_WALLED = ['door', 'window', 'board', 'screen'];
function stPlace(c, r, col, h, w, t) {
  const R = c.flip ? c.rows - r - h : r, C = c.flip ? c.cols - col - w : col;
  if (t && ST_WALLED.indexOf(t) >= 0) { const o = c.walls || {}; for (let y = r; y < r + h; y++) for (let x = col; x < col + w; x++) { const v = o[y + ',' + x]; if (v) return { R, C, side: c.flip ? { top: 'bottom', bottom: 'top', left: 'right', right: 'left' }[v] : v }; } }      /* the wall the teacher picked */
  const d = [['top', R], ['bottom', c.rows - R - h], ['left', C], ['right', c.cols - C - w]].sort((a, b) => a[1] - b[1] || ((w >= h) === (a[0] === 'top' || a[0] === 'bottom') ? -1 : 1));
  return { R, C, side: d[0][0] };
}
/* a door's hinge and swing: whichever of its squares has them */
/* which way a desk faces, in degrees clockwise from facing the front (older charts said up, down, left or right) */
const stDeg = f => typeof f === 'number' ? f : ({ down: 180, left: -90, right: 90 }[f] || 0);
/* door hinge and swing, or the way a desk faces */
const stOpt = (c, t, r, col, h, w) => t === 'door' ? stDoorOpt(c, r, col, h, w) : t === 'desk' ? { f: (c.face || {})[r + ',' + col] || 'up' } : null;
const stDoorOpt = (c, r, col, h, w) => { const o = c.doors || {}; for (let y = r; y < r + h; y++) for (let x = col; x < col + w; x++) if (o[y + ',' + x]) return o[y + ',' + x]; return null; };
/* on screen, which side of the square the chair is on (flip turns it around) */
/* on screen, where the chair (photo) and the desk top (name) are: offsets from the middle of the square, turned with the desk */
/* where a seat's photo (on the chair) and name (on the desk or table top) go: fractions of the square from its middle.
   Desks turn with their facing; table seats have their chair on the outside edge of the table; lab and computer seats face the front. */
function stSeatSpot(c, k) {
  const kind = (c.kinds || {})[k];
  if (kind === 'table') {
    const cells = stPieceAt(c, k).map(stRC), rs = cells.map(x => x[0]), cs = cells.map(x => x[1]), r0 = Math.min(...rs), r1 = Math.max(...rs), c0 = Math.min(...cs), c1 = Math.max(...cs), [r, col] = stRC(k);
    let side = r1 === r0 ? 'bottom' : r === r0 ? 'top' : r === r1 ? 'bottom' : col === c0 ? 'left' : col === c1 ? 'right' : '';
    if (c.flip) side = { top: 'bottom', bottom: 'top', left: 'right', right: 'left' }[side] || '';
    return { top: { fx: 0, fy: -.27, nx: 0, ny: .14 }, bottom: { fx: 0, fy: .27, nx: 0, ny: -.14 }, left: { fx: -.27, fy: -.1, nx: .06, ny: .28 }, right: { fx: .27, fy: -.1, nx: -.06, ny: .28 } }[side] || { fx: 0, fy: -.08, nx: 0, ny: .28 };
  }
  const deg = kind ? 0 : stDeg((c.face || {})[k]) + (c.flip ? 180 : 0), d = deg * Math.PI / 180, sn = Math.sin(d), cs = Math.cos(d);
  let nr = ((deg % 360) + 360) % 360; if (nr > 90 && nr < 270) nr -= 180; else if (nr >= 270) nr -= 360;      /* the name runs along the desk top, readable from the chair; a desk facing the back stays right side up */
  return { fx: -sn * .24, fy: cs * .23, nx: sn * .25, ny: -cs * .25, nr };
}
const stFaceShown = (c, k) => { const p = stSeatSpot(c, k), f = v => (v * 100).toFixed(1) + '%'; return [' tilt', `--fx:${f(p.fx)};--fy:${f(p.fy)};--nx:${f(p.nx)};--ny:${f(p.ny)};--nr:${p.nr || 0}deg;`]; };
/* the name on a desk: just the first name, with the last initial (or the rest of the name) only when two students in this class share that first name */
const stFirst = n => { const w = String(n || '').trim().split(/\s+/); return w.length > 1 ? w.slice(0, -1).join(' ') : w[0] || ''; };
function stDesk(st) {
  if (!st) return ''; const f = stFirst(st[1]).toLowerCase();
  return S.st.data.students.some(x => x[0] !== st[0] && stFirst(x[1]).toLowerCase() === f) ? st[1] : stFirst(st[1]);
}
const stArea = (R, C, h, w) => `grid-area:${R + 1} / ${C + 1} / span ${h} / span ${w}`;
const stPinSvg = '<svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" class="pin" aria-label="pinned"><circle cx="10" cy="7" r="5" fill="#D94F4F" stroke="#3D3328" stroke-width="1.5"/><line x1="10" y1="12" x2="10" y2="19" stroke="#3D3328" stroke-width="2"/></svg>';
function stGrid(big) {
  const c = S.st.chart, room = !big && S.st.mode === 'desks', seat = !big && S.st.mode === 'seat', out = [];
  for (let r = 0; r < c.rows; r++) for (let col = 0; col < c.cols; col++) { const p = stPlace(c, r, col, 1, 1); out.push(`<div class="stf" style="${stArea(p.R, p.C, 1, 1)}"></div>`); }
  stPieces(c).forEach(([t, r, col, h, w]) => { const p = stPlace(c, r, col, h, w, t), nm = (stSeatKind(t) || stThing(t) || [t, t])[1];
    out.push(`<div class="stp p-${t}" style="${stArea(p.R, p.C, h, w)}" title="${esc(nm)}">${stSvg(t, w, h, p.side, c.flip, null, stOpt(c, t, r, col, h, w))}</div>`); });
  const zonesOf = k => big ? '' : (c.zones || []).filter(z => z.cells.indexOf(k) >= 0).map(z => `<i style="background:${z.color}" title="${esc(z.name)}"></i>`).join('');
  for (let r = 0; r < c.rows; r++) for (let col = 0; col < c.cols; col++) {
    const k = r + ',' + col, p = stPlace(c, r, col, 1, 1), desk = c.desks.indexOf(k) >= 0, zd = zonesOf(k), dots = zd ? `<span class="stzd">${zd}</span>` : '';
    const off = desk && c.off.indexOf(k) >= 0;
    if (room) { const t = (c.items || {})[k], nm = t ? stThing(t)[1] : desk ? (stSeatKind(c.kinds[k]) || ST_SEATS[0])[1] + (off ? ', not in use' : '') : 'Empty floor'; out.push(`<div class="sthit${desk || t ? '' : ' open'}${off ? ' off' : ''}" data-k="${k}" style="${stArea(p.R, p.C, 1, 1)}" title="${esc(nm)}" aria-label="${esc(nm)}">${dots}</div>`); continue; }
    if (!desk) continue;
    if (off && !big) { out.push(`<div class="sts off" style="${stArea(p.R, p.C, 1, 1)}" title="Not in use" aria-label="Desk not in use"></div>`); continue; }
    const e = c.seats[k], st = e && S.st.data.students.find(x => x[0] === e);
    const pic = st ? (st[2] ? `<img src="${esc(st[2])}" alt="">` : `<span class="phin">${esc(phInit(st[1]))}</span>`) : '', isDesk = !(c.kinds || {})[k];
    const nm = stDesk(st);
    const face = !st ? '' : `<span class="stph">${pic}</span><span class="stnm" style="--nl:${Math.max(4, nm.length)}" title="${esc(st[1])}">${esc(nm)}</span>`;      /* photo on the chair, name on the desk or table top */
    if (big) { const fc = stFaceShown(c, k); out.push(`<div class="sts${fc[0]}${S.st.hl && e === S.st.hl ? ' hl' : ''}" data-k="${k}" style="${fc[1]}${stArea(p.R, p.C, 1, 1)}">${face}</div>`); continue; }
    const fc = stFaceShown(c, k);
    out.push(`<button class="sts${fc[0]}${S.st.pick === k ? ' sel' : ''}" data-a="stcell" data-k="${k}" style="${fc[1]}${stArea(p.R, p.C, 1, 1)}" aria-label="${st ? esc(st[1]) : 'Empty seat'}">${dots}${e && c.pins[e] === k ? stPinSvg : ''}${face}</button>`);
  }
  const front = '<div class="stfront">Front of the room</div>';
  return (c.flip ? '' : front) + `<div class="stfloor${room ? ' paint' : ''}" id="${big ? '' : 'stgrid'}" style="--sc:${c.cols};--sr:${c.rows}">${out.join('')}</div>` + (c.flip ? front : '');
}
/* step 2: each accommodation, the desks that meet it, and the students who need it */
/* step 2: every student and what they need. A need is an accommodation (desks the teacher marked) or a part of the room. */
function stNeedsCard(c) {
  const d = S.st.data, zs = c.zones || [];
  const sel = e => `<select data-e="${esc(e)}" class="stneedsel" aria-label="Add a need for ${esc(stName(e))}"><option value="">+ Add…</option><optgroup label="Classmates"><option value="near">Keep near…</option><option value="away">Keep away from…</option></optgroup>${zs.length ? `<optgroup label="Accommodations">${zs.map(z => `<option value="${esc(z.id)}">${esc(z.name)}</option>`).join('')}</optgroup>` : ''}<optgroup label="Part of the room">${ST_AUTO.map(a => `<option value="${a[0]}">${a[1]}</option>`).join('')}</optgroup><option value="new">New accommodation…</option></select>`;
  const chips = e => c.rules.filter(r => r.a === e || r.b === e).map(r => { const i = c.rules.indexOf(r), z = r.t === 'zone' && zs.find(x => x.id === r.z);
    const label = r.t === 'zone' ? (z ? `<i class="zsw" style="background:${z.color}"></i>` : '') + esc(stZoneName(c, r.z)) : (r.t === 'with' ? 'Near ' : 'Away from ') + esc(stName(r.a === e ? r.b : r.a));
    return `<span class="chip flat stneed">${label}<button class="link" data-a="strule-x" data-i="${i}" aria-label="Remove">✕</button></span>`; }).join('');
  return `<section class="card stack noprint" style="gap:8px"><div class="row"><h3 class="grow">${esc(S.st.cls)}: Students &amp; Their Needs</h3><span class="small muted">${d.students.length} students · Shuffle follows these</span></div>
    <div class="stneeds">${d.students.map(s => `<div class="stneedrow"><b>${esc(s[1])}</b><div class="wrap">${chips(s[0]) || '<span class="small muted">No needs</span>'}</div>${sel(s[0])}</div>`).join('')}</div>
    <p class="small muted" style="margin:0">Keep near means next to each other (side by side when possible); keep away means not next to each other, diagonals included. Accommodations are desks you mark (see below). Parts of the room are worked out for you.</p></section>`;
}
document.addEventListener('change', e => {
  const el = e.target; if (!el.classList || !el.classList.contains('stneedsel') || !el.value) return;
  const c = S.st.chart, a = el.dataset.e, z = el.value; el.value = '';
  if (z === 'new') { S.st.needFor = a; return ACT.stzonenew(); }
  if (z === 'near' || z === 'away') return modal(`<h2>${esc(stName(a))}: ${z === 'near' ? 'keep near' : 'keep away from'}…</h2><p class="muted">Pick a classmate.</p><div class="stpick">${S.st.data.students.filter(x => x[0] !== a).map(x => `<button class="chip flat" data-a="stpair" data-t="${z === 'near' ? 'with' : 'apart'}" data-a1="${esc(a)}" data-b="${esc(x[0])}">${esc(x[1])}</button>`).join('')}</div><button class="btn alt wide" data-a="close" style="margin-top:12px">Cancel</button>`);
  if (c.rules.some(r => r.t === 'zone' && r.a === a && r.z === z)) return toast('Already there.');
  c.rules.push({ t: 'zone', a, z }); if (!stZone(c, z).size) toast('No desks meet ' + stZoneName(c, z) + ' yet. Mark them in Room layout.'); stSave(); stPaint();
});
/* copy the furniture of one room into another class's chart: their students, Don't use desks, accommodations and rules stay */
const ST_FURN = ['rows', 'cols', 'desks', 'kinds', 'items', 'doors', 'walls', 'face'];
function stMergeRoom(src, t) {
  t = t || stNew(); ['desks', 'rules', 'zones', 'off'].forEach(k => { if (!Array.isArray(t[k])) t[k] = []; }); t.seats = t.seats || {}; t.pins = t.pins || {};
  ST_FURN.forEach(k => { t[k] = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(src[k] || (k === 'desks' ? [] : k === 'rows' || k === 'cols' ? src[k] : {}))); });
  const keep = k => t.desks.indexOf(k) >= 0;
  Object.keys(t.seats).forEach(k => { if (!keep(k)) { delete t.pins[t.seats[k]]; delete t.seats[k]; } });
  t.off = t.off.filter(keep); t.zones.forEach(z => { z.cells = z.cells.filter(keep); });
  return t;
}
ACT.stcopyto = () => { const d = S.st.data, others = d.classes.filter(x => x !== S.st.cls);
  modal(`<form class="stack" data-f="stcopyto"><h2>Copy This Room to Other Classes</h2><p class="muted">The desks and furniture from ${esc(S.st.cls)} replace the room in the classes you tick. Their students, Don’t use desks, accommodations and seats stay where the desks still are.</p>
    <div class="stack" style="gap:6px">${others.map(x => `<label class="row" style="gap:10px"><input type="checkbox" value="${esc(x)}" checked style="width:22px;height:22px"><span class="grow">${esc(x)}</span><span class="small muted">${(d.counts || {})[x] || 0} students</span></label>`).join('')}</div>
    <div class="row"><button type="button" class="btn alt grow" data-a="close">Cancel</button><button class="btn grow">Copy</button></div></form>`); };
FORM.stcopyto = async f => {
  const list = [...f.querySelectorAll('input:checked')].map(x => x.value); if (!list.length) return toast('Tick at least one class.');
  const b = f.querySelector('button.btn:not(.alt)'); b.disabled = true; b.textContent = 'Copying…';
  try { for (const cls of list) { const d = await call('api_seating', AUTH, cls); await call('api_seatingSave', AUTH, cls, stMergeRoom(S.st.chart, d.chart)); } closeModal(); toast('Copied to ' + list.length + (list.length === 1 ? ' class.' : ' classes.')); }
  catch (e) { b.disabled = false; b.textContent = 'Copy'; toast(e.message); }
};
ACT.stcopyfrom = () => { const d = S.st.data, others = d.classes.filter(x => x !== S.st.cls);
  modal(`<h2>Copy the Room From Another Class</h2><p class="muted">Its desks and furniture replace this room. ${esc(S.st.cls)} keeps its own students, Don’t use desks, accommodations and seats where the desks still are.</p><div class="stack" style="gap:8px">${others.map(x => `<button class="btn alt" data-a="stcopyfromgo" data-c="${esc(x)}">${esc(x)}</button>`).join('')}</div><button class="btn alt wide" data-a="close" style="margin-top:10px">Cancel</button>`); };
ACT.stcopyfromgo = el => { el.disabled = true; call('api_seating', AUTH, el.dataset.c).then(d => { if (!d.chart) { el.disabled = false; return toast('That class has no room yet.'); } closeModal(); stMergeRoom(d.chart, S.st.chart); stSave(); stPaint(); toast('Room copied from ' + el.dataset.c + '.'); }).catch(e => { el.disabled = false; toast(e.message); }); };
/* the How to use guide: opens by itself the first time, and from the ? button */
const ST_HELP = [
  ['Pick a class', 'Each class has its own chart. The buttons at the top show how many students each class has.'],
  ['1 · Room layout: build the room', 'Press Start over (under the tools) and pick a layout (Rows, Pairs, Groups of 4, Horseshoe, Semicircle) or Empty room. In the Furniture tab, pick a picture and tap a square, or drag across several. Seats are where students sit; Room things (including Work table) are never used for seating. Touching squares of the same thing join, like a double door or a big table. The Eraser removes things.'],
  ['Fine-tune it', 'In the Move & resize tab, Move drags a desk or piece to another square, and Row above and Column left make room anywhere (or use + Top, + Bottom, + Left, + Right). With Desk picked, choose which way desks face. With Door picked, choose the wall, the hinge side and the swing.'],
  ['Same room, different classes', 'Build the room once, then press Copy this room to other classes. Each class keeps its own students and settings, so in a class with 24 students you can mark 3 desks Don’t use, and in a class with 27 use them all.'],
  ['Who sits where', 'In the Who sits where tab, Don’t use greys out seats nobody should sit at (a desk or a table seat). New accommodation (for example Near the teacher, Hearing support) lets you tap the desks that meet it. Each class has its own.'],
  ['2 · Students & needs', 'Every student in the class is listed. Use + Add next to a name: Keep near or Keep away from a classmate, an accommodation (seats you mark, like Near the teacher), or a part of the room like Front two rows or Left side. Accommodations has + New accommodation and Mark desks.'],
  ['3 · Seat students', 'Shuffle seats everyone and keeps the needs and rules. Tap a student, then another seat, to swap. Pin keeps a student in place for the next Shuffle.'],
  ['Show it', 'Projector view fills the screen with names and photos only. Download picture saves a PNG. Print prints it. Flip view turns the room around. Nothing private (needs, pins, rules) shows in these.']
];
ACT.sthelp = () => modal(`<div class="stack" style="gap:12px"><h2 style="margin:0">How to Use the Seating Chart</h2><ol class="sthelp">${ST_HELP.map(h => `<li><b>${esc(h[0])}</b><span>${esc(h[1])}</span></li>`).join('')}</ol><p class="small muted" style="margin:0">Everything saves by itself. Only you see the seating chart.</p><button class="btn wide" data-a="close">Got it</button></div>`, true);
ACT.stpair = el => {
  const c = S.st.chart, t = el.dataset.t, a = el.dataset.a1, b = el.dataset.b, pair = r => r.t !== 'zone' && ((r.a === a && r.b === b) || (r.a === b && r.b === a));
  if (c.rules.some(r => pair(r) && r.t === t)) { closeModal(); return toast('That is already there.'); }
  if (c.rules.some(r => pair(r) && r.t !== t)) return toast('These two already have the opposite. Remove it first.');
  c.rules.push({ t, a, b }); closeModal(); stSave(); stPaint();
};
function stAccCard(c, opts) {
  const zs = c.zones || [];
  if (!zs.length) return '<section class="card stack noprint" style="gap:8px"><div class="row"><h3 class="grow">Accommodations</h3><button class="btn alt sm" data-a="stzonenew">+ New accommodation</button></div><p class="small muted" style="margin:0">None yet. An accommodation is a kind of seat some students need (for example Near the teacher or Hearing support). Make one, mark the desks that meet it, then add it to students above.</p></section>';
  return `<section class="card stack noprint" style="gap:12px"><div class="row"><h3 class="grow">Accommodations</h3><button class="btn alt sm" data-a="stzonenew">+ New accommodation</button></div>${zs.map(z => { const need = c.rules.filter(r => r.t === 'zone' && r.z === z.id), n = stZone(c, z.id).size;
    return `<div class="stacc"><div class="row" style="flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px"><i class="zsw" style="background:${z.color}"></i><b class="grow">${esc(z.name)}</b><span class="small muted">${n} ${n === 1 ? 'desk meets' : 'desks meet'} it</span><button class="btn alt sm" data-a="stmark" data-z="${esc(z.id)}">Mark desks</button><button class="link" data-a="stzonedel" data-z="${esc(z.id)}">Remove</button></div>
      <p class="small muted" style="margin:0">${need.length ? 'Needed by ' + need.map(r => esc(stName(r.a))).join(', ') : 'No students need it yet. Add it next to a name above.'}</p></div>`; }).join('')}</section>`;
}
FORM.stneed = f => {
  const c = S.st.chart, z = f.dataset.z, a = f.querySelector('select').value; if (!a) return;
  if (c.rules.some(r => r.t === 'zone' && r.a === a && r.z === z)) return toast('Already added.');
  c.rules.push({ t: 'zone', a, z }); if (!stZone(c, z).size) toast('No desks meet this yet. Tap them in Room layout.'); stSave(); stPaint();
};
ACT.stmark = el => { S.st.mode = 'desks'; S.st.tab = 'who'; S.st.tool = 'z:' + el.dataset.z; stPaint(); const g = $('#stgrid'); if (g) g.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center' }); toast('Tap the desks that meet it, then go to 2 · Students & needs.'); };
ACT.stzonedel = el => { const c = S.st.chart, z = el.dataset.z, m = c.zones.find(x => x.id === z); if (!m) return;
  modal(`<h2>Remove ${esc(m.name)}?</h2><p class="muted">The desks lose this mark and students who needed it lose that rule.</p><div class="row"><button class="btn alt grow" data-a="close">Cancel</button><button class="btn grow" data-a="stzonedelgo" data-z="${esc(z)}">Remove</button></div>`); };
ACT.stzonedelgo = el => { closeModal(); const c = S.st.chart, z = el.dataset.z; c.zones = c.zones.filter(x => x.id !== z); c.rules = c.rules.filter(r => !(r.t === 'zone' && r.z === z)); if (S.st.tool === 'z:' + z) S.st.tool = 'desk'; stSave(); stPaint(); };
const stIcon = t => `<span class="sticon">${stSvg(t, 1, 1, 'bottom')}</span>`;
/* the Room layout tool panel: three tabs of picture tiles, then the options for the chosen tool, then what tapping does now */
const ST_TABS = [['furn', 'Furniture', 'desk'], ['arrange', 'Move & resize', 'move'], ['who', 'Who sits where', 'off']];
function stRoomBar() {
  const c = S.st.chart, tool = S.st.tool || 'desk', on = t => tool === t ? ' sel' : '', tab = S.st.tab || 'furn';
  const tile = (t, nm, icon) => `<button class="sttile${on(t)}" data-a="sttool" data-t="${t}" aria-pressed="${tool === t}" title="${esc(nm)}"><span class="sti">${icon}</span><span class="stl">${esc(nm)}</span></button>`;
  const svgT = t => stSvg(t, 1, 1, 'bottom'), txt = x => `<span class="stglyph">${x}</span>`;
  const now = tool === 'off' ? '<b>Don’t use</b>: tap or drag across desks nobody should sit at. Do it again to use them.' : tool === 'move' ? 'nothing: <b>Move</b> is on. Press on a desk or any piece and drag it to another square. A single square dropped on another swaps them.' : tool === 'rowadd' ? 'a new row: tap a square to add an empty row above it.' : tool === 'coladd' ? 'a new column: tap a square to add an empty column to its left.' : tool === 'rowaddb' ? 'a new row: tap a square to add an empty row below it.' : tool === 'coladdr' ? 'a new column: tap a square to add an empty column to its right.' : tool === 'rowdel' ? 'nothing: tap a square to remove its whole row.' : tool === 'coldel' ? 'nothing: tap a square to remove its whole column.' : tool === 'erase' ? 'the Eraser: tap or drag to clear squares.' : /^z:/.test(tool) ? `<b>${esc(stZoneName(c, tool.slice(2)))}</b>: tap or drag across the seats that meet this accommodation (tap again to take one out).` : `<b>${esc((stSeatKind(tool) || stThing(tool) || ['', ''])[1])}</b>: tap a square, or drag across several.`;
  let body = '';
  if (tab === 'furn') body = `<div class="stgroup"><span class="stgh">Seats: students sit here</span><div class="sttiles">${ST_SEATS.map(t => tile(t[0], t[1], svgT(t[0]))).join('')}</div></div>
      <div class="stgroup"><span class="stgh">Room: no one sits here</span><div class="sttiles">${ST_THINGS.map(t => tile(t[0], t[1], svgT(t[0]))).join('')}${tile('erase', 'Eraser', txt('⌫'))}</div></div>`;
  else if (tab === 'arrange') body = `<div class="stgroup"><span class="stgh">Drag things around</span><div class="sttiles">${tile('move', 'Move', txt('✥'))}</div></div>
      <div class="stgroup"><span class="stgh">Add or remove space in the middle: pick one, then tap a square</span><div class="sttiles">${tile('rowadd', 'Row above', txt('⊕═'))}${tile('rowaddb', 'Row below', txt('═⊕'))}${tile('coladd', 'Column left', txt('⊕‖'))}${tile('coladdr', 'Column right', txt('‖⊕'))}${tile('rowdel', 'Remove row', txt('⊖═'))}${tile('coldel', 'Remove column', txt('⊖‖'))}</div></div>
      <div class="stgroup"><span class="stgh">Or at the edges</span>      <div class="row stsize" style="flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px"><span class="small muted"><b class="num">${c.rows}</b> rows</span><button class="chip flat" data-a="stedge" data-e="top" data-d="1">+ Top</button><button class="chip flat" data-a="stedge" data-e="bottom" data-d="1">+ Bottom</button><button class="chip flat" data-a="stedge" data-e="top" data-d="-1">− Top</button><button class="chip flat" data-a="stedge" data-e="bottom" data-d="-1">− Bottom</button>
        <span class="small muted" style="margin-left:10px"><b class="num">${c.cols}</b> columns</span><button class="chip flat" data-a="stedge" data-e="left" data-d="1">+ Left</button><button class="chip flat" data-a="stedge" data-e="right" data-d="1">+ Right</button><button class="chip flat" data-a="stedge" data-e="left" data-d="-1">− Left</button><button class="chip flat" data-a="stedge" data-e="right" data-d="-1">− Right</button></div></div>`;
  else body = `<div class="stgroup"><span class="stgh">Desks nobody should sit at</span><div class="sttiles">${tile('off', 'Don’t use', txt('⊘'))}</div></div>
      <div class="stgroup"><span class="stgh">Accommodations: pick one, then tap the desks that meet it</span><div class="sttiles">${(c.zones || []).map(z => tile('z:' + z.id, z.name, `<i class="zbig" style="background:${z.color}"></i>`)).join('')}<button class="sttile" data-a="stzonenew"><span class="sti">${txt('+')}</span><span class="stl">New accommodation</span></button></div></div>`;
  return `<section class="card stack stbar noprint" style="gap:10px">
    <div class="seg sttabs">${ST_TABS.map(t => `<button class="${tab === t[0] ? 'on' : ''}" data-a="sttab" data-t="${t[0]}">${t[1]}</button>`).join('')}</div>
    ${body}
    ${tool === 'desk' ? (() => { const f = stDeg(S.st.face), b = (v, l, t) => `<button class="${f === v ? 'on' : ''}" data-a="stface" data-v="${v}" title="${t}" aria-label="${t}">${l}</button>`;
      return `<div class="row" style="flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px"><span class="small muted">Desks face</span><div class="seg stdirs">${b(-45, '↖', 'Front left')}${b(0, '↑', 'Front')}${b(45, '↗', 'Front right')}${b(-90, '←', 'Left')}${b(90, '→', 'Right')}${b(-135, '↙', 'Back left')}${b(180, '↓', 'Back')}${b(135, '↘', 'Back right')}</div><button class="chip flat" data-a="stfaceall">Turn every desk this way</button><button class="chip flat" data-a="staim">Point every desk at the front</button><span class="small muted grow">To turn one desk, pick a direction, then tap it.</span></div>`; })() : ''}
    ${ST_WALLED.indexOf(tool) >= 0 ? (() => { const w = S.st.wall || 'auto', b = (v, l) => `<button class="${w === v ? 'on' : ''}" data-a="stwall" data-v="${v}">${l}</button>`;
      return `<div class="row" style="flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px"><span class="small muted">On the wall</span><div class="seg">${b('auto', 'Nearest')}${b('top', 'Top')}${b('bottom', 'Bottom')}${b('left', 'Left')}${b('right', 'Right')}</div><span class="small muted grow">Top is the front of the room. To move one that is already placed, pick a wall, then tap it.</span></div>`; })() : ''}
    ${tool === 'door' ? (() => { const o = S.st.door || { h: 'right', s: 'in' }, b = (k, v, l) => `<button class="${o[k] === v ? 'on' : ''}" data-a="stdoor" data-k="${k}" data-v="${v}">${l}</button>`;
      return `<div class="row" style="flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px"><span class="small muted">Hinge</span><div class="seg">${b('h', 'left', 'Left')}${b('h', 'right', 'Right')}</div><span class="small muted">Opens</span><div class="seg">${b('s', 'in', 'Into the room')}${b('s', 'out', 'Out of the room')}</div><span class="small muted grow">Left and right as you face the door from inside. To change a door, pick these, then tap it.</span></div>`; })() : ''}
    <p class="stnow" style="margin:0">Now: ${now}</p>
  </section>`;
}
ACT.sttab = el => { const t = ST_TABS.find(x => x[0] === el.dataset.t); S.st.tab = t[0]; S.st.tool = t[2]; stPaint(); };
function stPaint() {
  const b = $('#stbody'), d = S.st.data, c = S.st.chart; if (!b || !c) return;
  if (!d.students.length) { b.innerHTML = '<section class="card"><p>No students in this class yet.</p></section>'; return; }
  const seated = new Set(Object.values(c.seats)), loose = d.students.filter(s => !seated.has(s[0])), m = S.st.mode, opts = d.students.map(s => `<option value="${esc(s[0])}">${esc(s[1])}</option>`).join('');
  const zopts = `<optgroup label="Accommodations">${(c.zones || []).map(z => `<option value="${esc(z.id)}">${esc(z.name)}</option>`).join('') || '<option value="" disabled>None yet: add one in Room layout</option>'}</optgroup><optgroup label="Parts of the room">${ST_AUTO.map(a => `<option value="${a[0]}">${a[1]}</option>`).join('')}</optgroup>`;
  const pickE = c.seats[S.st.pick] || (S.st.pick && S.st.pick.indexOf('@') > 0 ? S.st.pick : '');
  const rule = r => `<li><span class="grow">${r.t === 'zone' ? `<b>${esc(stName(r.a))}</b> sits in: ${esc(stZoneName(c, r.z))}` : `<b>${esc(stName(r.a))}</b> and <b>${esc(stName(r.b))}</b> ${r.t === 'with' ? 'sit together' : 'sit apart'}`}</span><button class="link" data-a="strule-x" data-i="${c.rules.indexOf(r)}" aria-label="Remove this rule">Remove</button></li>`;
  const short = stUsable(c).length < d.students.length ? `<p class="small" style="margin:0;color:#D64545"><b>${d.students.length - stUsable(c).length} more seats needed</b> for everyone in this class.</p>` : '';
  const step = (k, n, t) => `<button class="${m === k ? 'on' : ''}" data-a="stmode" data-m="${k}"><b>${n}</b> ${t}</button>`;
  b.innerHTML = `<div class="stack">
    ${d.classes.length > 1 ? `<div class="seg stclasses" style="flex-wrap:wrap">${d.classes.map(x => `<button class="${x === S.st.cls ? 'on' : ''}" data-a="stcls" data-c="${esc(x)}">${esc(x)}<small>${(d.counts || {})[x] || 0} students</small></button>`).join('')}</div>` : ''}
    <div class="row noprint" style="flex-wrap:wrap"><button class="btn alt sm" data-a="sthelp">? How to use</button><div class="seg ststeps">${step('desks', '1', 'Room layout')}${step('needs', '2', 'Students & needs')}${step('seat', '3', 'Seat students')}</div><span class="grow"></span><span class="small muted" id="stsaved"></span></div>
    ${m === 'desks' ? `${stRoomBar()}
      ${d.classes.length > 1 ? `<div class="row noprint stcopy" style="flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px"><span class="small muted">Same room for other classes?</span><button class="chip flat" data-a="stcopyto">Copy this room to other classes…</button><button class="chip flat" data-a="stcopyfrom">Copy the room from…</button><span class="small muted grow">Only the furniture is copied. Each class keeps its own students, Don’t use desks, accommodations and seats.</span></div>` : ''}
      <div class="row noprint" style="flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px"><span class="grow"></span><span class="small muted">Start over:</span>${ST_LAYOUTS.map(o => `<button class="chip flat" data-a="stlayout" data-l="${o[0]}">${o[1]}</button>`).join('')}</div>
      <section class="card stprint">${stGrid(false)}</section>
      <div class="row noprint" style="flex-wrap:wrap"><p class="small muted grow" style="margin:0">${stUsable(c).length} seats in use for ${d.students.length} students${c.off.length ? ` (${c.off.length} marked Don’t use)` : ''}. Touching squares of the same thing join into one piece. Changes save by themselves.</p><button class="btn sun" data-a="stmode" data-m="needs">Next: students & needs →</button></div>${short}`
    : m === 'needs' ? `${stNeedsCard(c)}${stAccCard(c)}<div class="row noprint" style="flex-wrap:wrap"><button class="btn alt" data-a="stmode" data-m="desks">← Room layout</button><span class="grow"></span><button class="btn sun" data-a="stmode" data-m="seat">Next: seat students →</button></div>`
    : `<section class="card stack noprint" style="gap:10px"><div class="row" style="flex-wrap:wrap"><button class="btn sun" data-a="stshuffle">Shuffle</button><button class="btn alt" data-a="staz" data-by="first">A–Z first name</button><button class="btn alt" data-a="staz" data-by="last">A–Z last name</button><button class="btn alt" data-a="stclear">Clear seats</button>${pickE ? `<button class="btn alt" data-a="stpin">${c.pins[pickE] ? 'Unpin' : 'Pin'} ${esc(stName(pickE))}</button>` : ''}<span class="grow"></span><button class="btn alt" data-a="stflip">Flip view</button><button class="btn alt" data-a="stfull">Projector view</button><button class="btn alt" data-a="stpng">Download picture</button><button class="btn alt" data-a="stprint">Print</button></div>
      <p class="small muted" style="margin:0">Tap a student, then another seat, to swap them. Tap a name under Not seated, then an empty seat. Pinned students stay put when you shuffle. A–Z seats everyone in name order, front row first, left to right (handy for a study hall).</p>${short}</section>
      <section class="card stprint">${stGrid(false)}</section>
      ${loose.length ? `<section class="card stack noprint" style="gap:8px"><h3>Not Seated (${loose.length})</h3><div class="wrap">${loose.map(s => `<button class="chip flat${S.st.pick === s[0] ? ' sel' : ''}" data-a="stloose" data-e="${esc(s[0])}">${esc(s[1])}</button>`).join('')}</div></section>` : ''}
      <p class="small muted noprint" style="margin:0">${c.rules.length ? c.rules.length + (c.rules.length === 1 ? ' need or rule is' : ' needs and rules are') + ' followed by Shuffle.' : 'No needs yet.'} Change them in <button class="link" data-a="stmode" data-m="needs">2 · Students & needs</button>.</p>`}
  </div>`;
  const t = $('#strt'), bsel = $('#strb'), zsel = $('#strz'); if (t && bsel) { const sync = () => { bsel.hidden = t.value === 'zone'; zsel.hidden = t.value !== 'zone'; }; t.addEventListener('change', sync); sync(); }
  if (m === 'desks') stPaintWire();
}
/* Room layout: tap or drag; every square the pointer crosses gets the chosen tool. An area turns on or off for the whole drag, decided by the first seat. */
function stPaintWire() {
  const g = $('#stgrid'); if (!g) return;
  let on = false, done = null, add = true, lx = 0, ly = 0;
  const at = e => { const el = document.elementFromPoint(e.clientX, e.clientY), cell = el && el.closest('[data-k]'); return cell && g.contains(cell) ? cell.dataset.k : ''; };
  const hit = k => { if (!k || done.has(k)) return; done.add(k); stApply(k, add); const cell = g.querySelector('[data-k="' + k + '"]'); if (cell) cell.classList.add('hit'); };
  if (S.st.tool === 'move') return stMoveWire(g, at);
  if (/^(row|col)(add|addb|addr|del)$/.test(S.st.tool)) { g.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => { const k = at(e); if (!k) return; e.preventDefault(); stSpace(S.st.tool, k); }); return; }
  g.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => { const k = at(e); if (!k) return; e.preventDefault(); on = true; done = new Set(); lx = e.clientX; ly = e.clientY;
    const z = /^z:/.test(S.st.tool) && (S.st.chart.zones || []).find(x => 'z:' + x.id === S.st.tool); add = S.st.tool === 'off' ? S.st.chart.off.indexOf(k) < 0 : !z || z.cells.indexOf(k) < 0; try { g.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (x) {} hit(k); });
  g.addEventListener('pointermove', e => { if (!on) return; const dx = e.clientX - lx, dy = e.clientY - ly, n = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Math.hypot(dx, dy) / 12)); for (let i = 1; i <= n; i++) hit(at({ clientX: lx + dx * i / n, clientY: ly + dy * i / n })); lx = e.clientX; ly = e.clientY; });
  const end = () => { if (!on) return; on = false; stSave(); stPaint(); };
  g.addEventListener('pointerup', end); g.addEventListener('pointercancel', end);
}
/* the chosen tool on one square (never toggles: only the eraser removes; an area adds or removes for the whole drag) */
function stApply(k, add) {
  const c = S.st.chart, tool = S.st.tool || 'desk', desk = c.desks.indexOf(k) >= 0;
  const dropDesk = () => { if (c.desks.indexOf(k) < 0) return; c.desks.splice(c.desks.indexOf(k), 1); c.off = c.off.filter(x => x !== k); const e = c.seats[k]; delete c.seats[k]; if (e) delete c.pins[e]; delete c.kinds[k]; delete c.face[k]; c.zones.forEach(z => { z.cells = z.cells.filter(x => x !== k); }); };
  if (tool === 'erase') { dropDesk(); delete c.items[k]; delete c.doors[k]; delete c.walls[k]; return; }
  if (tool === 'off') { if (!desk) return; const i = c.off.indexOf(k); if (add && i < 0) { c.off.push(k); const e = c.seats[k]; if (e) { delete c.seats[k]; delete c.pins[e]; } } if (!add && i >= 0) c.off.splice(i, 1); return; }
  if (/^z:/.test(tool)) { const z = c.zones.find(x => 'z:' + x.id === tool); if (!z || !desk) return; const i = z.cells.indexOf(k); if (add && i < 0) z.cells.push(k); if (!add && i >= 0) z.cells.splice(i, 1); return; }
  if (stThing(tool)) { dropDesk(); c.items[k] = tool; if (ST_WALLED.indexOf(tool) >= 0 && S.st.wall && S.st.wall !== 'auto') c.walls[k] = S.st.wall; else delete c.walls[k]; if (tool === 'door') c.doors[k] = Object.assign({}, S.st.door || { h: 'right', s: 'in' }); else delete c.doors[k]; return; }
  delete c.items[k]; delete c.doors[k]; delete c.walls[k]; if (!desk) c.desks.push(k); if (tool === 'desk') { delete c.kinds[k]; const f = stDeg(S.st.face); if (!f) delete c.face[k]; else c.face[k] = f; } else { c.kinds[k] = tool; delete c.face[k]; }
}

  <label class="field" for="stzn">Accommodation<input id="stzn" type="text" maxlength="30" placeholder="for example Near the teacher" required></label>
  <div class="wrap">${['Near the teacher', 'Hearing support', 'Vision support', 'Easy exit', 'Wheelchair access', 'Left-handed desk', 'Quiet spot', 'Near an outlet'].map(x => `<button type="button" class="chip flat" data-a="stzpick" data-v="${x}">${x}</button>`).join('')}</div>
  <div class="row"><button type="button" class="btn alt grow" data-a="close">Cancel</button><button class="btn grow">Add</button></div></form>`);

ACT.stmode = el => { S.st.mode = el.dataset.m; S.st.pick = ''; stPaint(); const b = $('#stbody'); if (b) b.scrollIntoView({ block: 'start' }); };
ACT.stcell = el => {
  const c = S.st.chart, k = el.dataset.k; if (S.st.mode !== 'seat' || c.desks.indexOf(k) < 0 || c.off.indexOf(k) >= 0) return;
  const p = S.st.pick;
  if (!p) { if (c.seats[k]) S.st.pick = k; return stPaint(); }
  if (p === k) { S.st.pick = ''; return stPaint(); }
  if (p.indexOf('@') > 0 || !/^\d+,\d+$/.test(p)) { const was = c.seats[k]; if (was) delete c.pins[was]; c.seats[k] = p; }      /* from Not seated; whoever sat here goes there */
  else { const x = c.seats[p], y = c.seats[k]; if (y) c.seats[p] = y; else delete c.seats[p]; if (x) c.seats[k] = x; else delete c.seats[k]; if (x) delete c.pins[x]; if (y) delete c.pins[y]; }
  S.st.pick = ''; stSave(); stPaint();
};

ACT.stclear = () => { const c = S.st.chart; Object.keys(c.seats).forEach(k => { if (c.pins[c.seats[k]] !== k) delete c.seats[k]; }); S.st.pick = ''; stSave(); stPaint(); };

  modal(`<h2>Start Over With ${esc((ST_LAYOUTS.find(o => o[0] === el.dataset.l) || ['', 'a new layout'])[1])}?</h2><p class="muted">This clears the room and everyone goes back to Not seated. Your rules and seat-area names stay.</p><div class="row"><button class="btn alt grow" data-a="close">Cancel</button><button class="btn grow" data-a="stlayoutgo" data-l="${el.dataset.l}">Start over</button></div>`);

ACT.stlayoutgo = el => { closeModal(); const c = S.st.chart; Object.assign(c, { seats: {}, pins: {}, items: {}, kinds: {}, face: {}, doors: {}, walls: {}, off: [] }, stLayout(el.dataset.l)); c.zones.forEach(z => { z.cells = []; }); stSave(); stPaint(); };
/* every square-based part of the chart moved by fn(row, col) -> [row, col], or dropped when fn gives null */
function stRemap(c, fn) {
  const m = k => { const [r, col] = stRC(k), t = fn(r, col); return t ? t[0] + ',' + t[1] : null; };
  c.desks = c.desks.map(m).filter(Boolean); c.off = (c.off || []).map(m).filter(Boolean);
  ['kinds', 'items', 'doors', 'face', 'walls'].forEach(n => { const o = {}; Object.keys(c[n] || {}).forEach(k => { const k2 = m(k); if (k2) o[k2] = c[n][k]; }); c[n] = o; });
  const seats = {}; Object.keys(c.seats).forEach(k => { const k2 = m(k); if (k2) seats[k2] = c.seats[k]; else delete c.pins[c.seats[k]]; }); c.seats = seats;
  Object.keys(c.pins).forEach(e => { const k2 = m(c.pins[e]); if (k2) c.pins[e] = k2; else delete c.pins[e]; });
  c.zones.forEach(z => { z.cells = z.cells.map(m).filter(Boolean); });
}
/* seats a student can sit in: desks not marked Don't use */
const stUsable = c => c.desks.filter(k => (c.off || []).indexOf(k) < 0);
const stHas = (c, k) => c.desks.indexOf(k) >= 0 || !!(c.items || {})[k];
/* add or remove a row or column: at an edge (stedge) or through one square (Space tools). Removing asks first when something is in the way. */
function stRowCol(kind, at, add, ask) {
  const c = S.st.chart, row = kind === 'row';
  if (add && (row ? c.rows : c.cols) >= 16) return toast('16 is the most ' + (row ? 'rows' : 'columns') + '.');
  if (!add && (row ? c.rows : c.cols) <= 1) return;
  if (!add && ask) { const n = c.desks.concat(Object.keys(c.items)).filter(k => stRC(k)[row ? 0 : 1] === at).length;
    if (n) return modal(`<h2>Remove This ${row ? 'row' : 'column'}?</h2><p class="muted">${n} ${n === 1 ? 'thing is' : 'things are'} in it and will be removed too.</p><div class="row"><button class="btn alt grow" data-a="close">Cancel</button><button class="btn grow" data-a="stlinego" data-k="${kind}" data-at="${at}">Remove</button></div>`); }
  stRemap(c, (r, col) => { const v = row ? r : col; if (!add && v === at) return null; const nv = add ? (v >= at ? v + 1 : v) : (v > at ? v - 1 : v); return row ? [nv, col] : [r, nv]; });
  if (row) c.rows += add ? 1 : -1; else c.cols += add ? 1 : -1;
  stSave(); stPaint();
}
ACT.stlinego = el => { closeModal(); stRowCol(el.dataset.k, +el.dataset.at, false, false); };
ACT.stedge = el => { const c = S.st.chart, e = el.dataset.e, add = el.dataset.d === '1', row = e === 'top' || e === 'bottom';
  stRowCol(row ? 'row' : 'col', e === 'top' || e === 'left' ? 0 : add ? (row ? c.rows : c.cols) : (row ? c.rows : c.cols) - 1, add, true); };
function stSpace(tool, k) { const [r, col] = stRC(k), row = tool.indexOf('row') === 0, after = /add[br]$/.test(tool) ? 1 : 0; stRowCol(row ? 'row' : 'col', (row ? r : col) + after, /add/.test(tool), true); }      /* Row below / Column right add after the tapped square */
/* Move: press on a piece, drag, let go on another square. A whole joined piece (a table, a double door) moves together. */
function stTake(c, k) {
  const o = { off: c.off.indexOf(k) >= 0, desk: c.desks.indexOf(k) >= 0, kind: c.kinds[k], face: c.face[k], item: c.items[k], door: c.doors[k], wall: c.walls[k], seat: c.seats[k], zones: c.zones.filter(z => z.cells.indexOf(k) >= 0).map(z => z.id) };
  o.pin = o.seat && c.pins[o.seat] === k;
  if (o.desk) c.desks.splice(c.desks.indexOf(k), 1); c.off = c.off.filter(x => x !== k); ['kinds', 'face', 'items', 'doors', 'walls', 'seats'].forEach(n => { delete c[n][k]; }); c.zones.forEach(z => { z.cells = z.cells.filter(x => x !== k); });
  return o;
}
function stPut(c, k, o) {
  if (o.desk) c.desks.push(k); if (o.off) c.off.push(k); [['kind', 'kinds'], ['face', 'face'], ['item', 'items'], ['door', 'doors'], ['wall', 'walls'], ['seat', 'seats']].forEach(([a, n]) => { if (o[a] != null) c[n][k] = o[a]; });
  if (o.seat && o.pin) c.pins[o.seat] = k; c.zones.forEach(z => { if (o.zones.indexOf(z.id) >= 0) z.cells.push(k); });
}
const stPieceAt = (c, k) => { const [r, col] = stRC(k), p = stPieces(c).find(([t, pr, pc, h, w]) => r >= pr && r < pr + h && col >= pc && col < pc + w); const out = []; if (p) for (let y = p[1]; y < p[1] + p[3]; y++) for (let x = p[2]; x < p[2] + p[4]; x++) out.push(y + ',' + x); return out; };
function stMoveTo(src, dst) {
  const c = S.st.chart, cells = stPieceAt(c, src); if (!cells.length || src === dst) return;
  const [r1, c1] = stRC(src), [r2, c2] = stRC(dst), dr = r2 - r1, dc = c2 - c1, to = cells.map(k => { const [r, col] = stRC(k); return [r + dr, col + dc]; });
  if (to.some(([r, col]) => r < 0 || col < 0 || r >= c.rows || col >= c.cols)) return toast('That would go off the edge of the room. Add a row or column first.');
  const dk = to.map(x => x[0] + ',' + x[1]);
  if (cells.length === 1) { const a = stTake(c, src), b = stTake(c, dst); stPut(c, dst, a); stPut(c, src, b); return; }      /* one square: swap */
  if (dk.some(k => cells.indexOf(k) < 0 && stHas(c, k))) return toast('Something is in the way. Move it or clear those squares first.');
  const taken = cells.map(k => stTake(c, k)); taken.forEach((o, i) => stPut(c, dk[i], o));
}
function stMoveWire(g, at) {
  let src = '', cells = [], tgt = '';
  const mark = () => { g.querySelectorAll('.lift, .drop').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('lift', 'drop')); cells.forEach(k => { const el = g.querySelector('[data-k="' + k + '"]'); if (el) el.classList.add('lift'); });
    if (tgt && src) { const [r1, c1] = stRC(src), [r2, c2] = stRC(tgt); cells.forEach(k => { const [r, col] = stRC(k), el = g.querySelector('[data-k="' + (r + r2 - r1) + ',' + (col + c2 - c1) + '"]'); if (el) el.classList.add('drop'); }); } };
  g.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => { const k = at(e); if (!k) return; e.preventDefault(); if (!stHas(S.st.chart, k)) return toast('Press on a desk or another piece, then drag it.'); src = k; tgt = ''; cells = stPieceAt(S.st.chart, k); try { g.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (x) {} mark(); });
  g.addEventListener('pointermove', e => { if (!src) return; const k = at(e); if (k && k !== tgt) { tgt = k; mark(); } });
  const end = () => { if (!src) return; const a = src, b = tgt; src = ''; cells = []; tgt = ''; if (b && b !== a) { stMoveTo(a, b); stSave(); } stPaint(); };
  g.addEventListener('pointerup', end); g.addEventListener('pointercancel', end);
}
ACT.stsize = el => {
  const c = S.st.chart; c.rows = Math.max(1, Math.min(16, c.rows + +(el.dataset.r || 0))); c.cols = Math.max(1, Math.min(16, c.cols + +(el.dataset.c || 0)));
  const keep = k => { const [r, col] = stRC(k); return r < c.rows && col < c.cols; };
  c.desks = c.desks.filter(keep); c.zones.forEach(z => { z.cells = z.cells.filter(keep); });
  Object.keys(c.items).forEach(k => { if (!keep(k)) delete c.items[k]; }); Object.keys(c.kinds).forEach(k => { if (!keep(k)) delete c.kinds[k]; });
  Object.keys(c.seats).forEach(k => { if (!keep(k)) { delete c.pins[c.seats[k]]; delete c.seats[k]; } });
  stSave(); stPaint();
};
FORM.strule = f => {
  const t = $('#strt', f).value, a = $('#stra', f).value, b = $('#strb', f).value, z = $('#strz', f).value, c = S.st.chart;
  if (t === 'zone') { if (!z) return toast('Pick a seat area.'); if (c.rules.some(r => r.t === 'zone' && r.a === a && r.z === z)) return toast('That rule is already there.'); if (!stZone(c, z).size) toast('There are no seats in ' + stZoneName(c, z) + ' yet.'); c.rules.push({ t, a, z }); stSave(); return stPaint(); }
  if (a === b) return toast('Pick two different students.');
  const pair = r => r.t !== 'zone' && ((r.a === a && r.b === b) || (r.a === b && r.b === a));
  if (c.rules.some(r => pair(r) && r.t === t)) return toast('That rule is already there.');
  if (c.rules.some(r => pair(r) && r.t !== t)) return toast('These two already have the opposite rule. Remove it first.');
  c.rules.push({ t, a, b }); stSave(); stPaint();
};
ACT['strule-x'] = el => { S.st.chart.rules.splice(+el.dataset.i, 1); stSave(); stPaint(); };
/* how badly a seating breaks the rules: 0 means every rule is kept */
function stCost(chart, at, zs) {
  let cost = 0; const bad = [];
  chart.rules.forEach(r => {
    const a = at[r.a], b = at[r.b];
    if (r.t === 'zone') { const z = zs ? zs[r.z] : stZone(chart, r.z); if (a && !z.has(a)) { cost += 10; bad.push(r); } return; }
    if (!a || !b) return;
    if (r.t === 'with') { if (!stNear(a, b)) { cost += 10; bad.push(r); } else if (!stSide(a, b)) cost += 1; }
    if (r.t === 'apart' && stNear(a, b)) { cost += 10; bad.push(r); }
  });
  return { cost, bad };
}
/* random seats (students with an area rule first), then many small swaps that never make it worse; the best of several tries wins */
function stSolve(chart, emails) {
  const zs = {}; chart.rules.forEach(r => { if (r.t === 'zone' && !zs[r.z]) zs[r.z] = stZone(chart, r.z); });
  const pins = chart.pins, pinned = new Set(Object.values(pins)), free = stUsable(chart).filter(k => !pinned.has(k)), movers = emails.filter(e => !pins[e]);
  const want = {}; chart.rules.forEach(r => { if (r.t === 'zone') (want[r.a] = want[r.a] || []).push(zs[r.z]); });
  let best = null;
  for (let tryN = 0; tryN < 12; tryN++) {
    const at = {}; Object.keys(pins).forEach(e => { at[e] = pins[e]; });
    let open = shuffle(free.slice());
    shuffle(movers.slice()).sort((x, y) => (want[y] ? 1 : 0) - (want[x] ? 1 : 0)).forEach(e => {
      const fit = want[e] ? open.find(k => want[e].every(z => z.has(k))) || open.find(k => want[e].some(z => z.has(k))) : null, k = fit || open[0];
      if (k) { at[e] = k; open = open.filter(x => x !== k); }
    });
    const who = {}; Object.keys(at).forEach(e => { who[at[e]] = e; });
    let cur = stCost(chart, at, zs).cost;
    for (let i = 0; i < 3000 && cur > 0; i++) {
      const e = movers[Math.floor(Math.random() * movers.length)], k = free[Math.floor(Math.random() * free.length)]; if (!e || !k || !at[e] || at[e] === k) continue;
      const from = at[e], o = who[k];
      at[e] = k; who[k] = e; if (o) { at[o] = from; who[from] = o; } else delete who[from];
      const nc = stCost(chart, at, zs).cost;
      if (nc <= cur) cur = nc; else { at[e] = from; who[from] = e; if (o) { at[o] = k; who[k] = o; } else delete who[k]; }
    }
    if (!best || cur < best.cost) best = { cost: cur, at: Object.assign({}, at) };
    if (cur === 0) break;
  }
  const seats = {}; Object.keys(best.at).forEach(e => { seats[best.at[e]] = e; });
  return { seats, bad: stCost(chart, best.at, zs).bad };
}
/* A–Z: everyone in name order, front row first, left to right as the chart is drawn (Front of the room at the top),
   skipping Don't use desks. Pinned students stay put; seat rules are not used. For classes like a study hall,
   where quick attendance matters more than who sits by whom. */
const stLast = n => { const w = String(n || '').trim().split(/\s+/); return w[w.length - 1] || ''; };
ACT.staz = el => {
  const c = S.st.chart, last = el.dataset.by === 'last', key = s => last ? stLast(s[1]) + ' ' + stFirst(s[1]) : s[1], seats = {};
  Object.keys(c.seats).forEach(k => { const e = c.seats[k]; if (c.pins[e] === k) seats[k] = e; });
  const kept = new Set(Object.values(seats));
  const free = stUsable(c).filter(k => !seats[k]).sort((a, b) => { const [r1, c1] = stRC(a), [r2, c2] = stRC(b); return r1 - r2 || c1 - c2; });
  const list = S.st.data.students.filter(s => !kept.has(s[0])).sort((a, b) => key(a).localeCompare(key(b), undefined, { sensitivity: 'base' }));
  list.forEach((s, i) => { if (free[i]) seats[free[i]] = s[0]; });
  c.seats = seats; S.st.pick = ''; stSave(); stPaint();
  const left = Math.max(0, list.length - free.length);
  toast(left ? left + ' students have no seat. Add seats in Room layout.' : 'Seated A to Z by ' + (last ? 'last' : 'first') + ' name' + (kept.size ? '. Pinned students stayed put.' : '.'));
};
ACT.stshuffle = () => {
  const c = S.st.chart, emails = S.st.data.students.map(s => s[0]), r = stSolve(c, emails); c.seats = r.seats; S.st.pick = ''; stSave(); stPaint();
  const left = emails.length - Object.keys(r.seats).length;
  if (r.bad.length) modal(`<h2>Almost</h2><p class="muted">Everyone is seated${left ? `, except ${left} without a seat` : ''}, but ${r.bad.length === 1 ? 'one rule could not be kept' : r.bad.length + ' rules could not be kept'}:</p><ul>${r.bad.map(x => `<li>${x.t === 'zone' ? esc(stName(x.a)) + ' sits in: ' + esc(stZoneName(c, x.z)) : esc(stName(x.a)) + ' and ' + esc(stName(x.b)) + (x.t === 'with' ? ' together' : ' apart')}</li>`).join('')}</ul><p class="small muted">Try Shuffle again, add seats to the area, or move these students by hand.</p><button class="btn wide" data-a="close">OK</button>`);
  else toast(left ? left + ' students have no seat. Add seats in Room layout.' : c.rules.length ? 'Shuffled. Every rule is kept.' : 'Shuffled.');
};
ACT.stfull = () => {
  if ($('#stfull')) return;
  const d = document.createElement('div'); d.id = 'stfull'; d.setAttribute('role', 'dialog'); d.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Seating chart, projector view');
  const draw = () => `<div class="row"><h2 class="grow">${esc(S.st.cls)}</h2><button class="btn alt" data-a="stfullflip">Flip</button><button class="btn alt" data-a="stfullx">Exit projector view</button></div><div class="stfullgrid">${stGrid(true)}</div>`;
