  try { const p = document.documentElement.requestFullscreen && document.documentElement.requestFullscreen(); if (p && p.catch) p.catch(() => {}); } catch (e) {}
};
/* the chart as a PNG picture: the room drawings, then photos (or initials) and names on the seats; nothing private */
async function stPng() {
  const c = S.st.chart, cw = 150, ch = 142, gap = 10, pad = 30, head = 100, foot = 56, W = pad * 2 + c.cols * cw + (c.cols - 1) * gap, H = head + c.rows * ch + (c.rows - 1) * gap + foot;
  const cv = document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width = W; cv.height = H; const x = cv.getContext('2d'), top = c.flip ? 24 : head;
  const load = src => new Promise(ok => { const im = new Image(); im.onload = () => ok(im); im.onerror = () => ok(null); im.src = src; });
  const pics = {}; await Promise.all(S.st.data.students.filter(st => st[2]).map(st => load(st[2]).then(im => { if (im) pics[st[0]] = im; })));
  x.fillStyle = '#fff'; x.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  x.fillStyle = '#1F2340'; x.font = '600 30px Fraunces, Georgia, serif'; x.textBaseline = 'middle'; x.fillText(S.st.cls, pad, 34);
  const fy = c.flip ? H - foot + 14 : 60; x.fillStyle = '#EEF0F5'; x.beginPath(); x.roundRect(pad, fy, W - pad * 2, 28, 8); x.fill(); x.fillStyle = '#6B6F86'; x.font = '800 15px Nunito, Arial, sans-serif'; x.textAlign = 'center'; x.fillText('FRONT OF THE ROOM', W / 2, fy + 15); x.textAlign = 'left';
  for (const [t, r, col, h, w] of stPieces(c)) {
    const p = stPlace(c, r, col, h, w, t), pw = w * cw + (w - 1) * gap, ph = h * ch + (h - 1) * gap;
    const im = await load('data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,' + encodeURIComponent(stSvg(t, w, h, p.side, c.flip, [pw, ph], stOpt(c, t, r, col, h, w))));
    if (im) x.drawImage(im, pad + p.C * (cw + gap), top + p.R * (ch + gap), pw, ph);
  }
  /* desks marked Don't use look like any empty desk in the picture: that mark is for the teacher only */
  c.desks.forEach(k => {
    const e = c.seats[k], st = e && S.st.data.students.find(s => s[0] === e); if (!st) return;
    const [r, col] = stRC(k), p = stPlace(c, r, col, 1, 1), X = pad + p.C * (cw + gap), Y = top + p.R * (ch + gap), sp = stSeatSpot(c, k), rad = 34;
    const cx = X + cw / 2 + sp.fx * cw, cy = Y + ch / 2 + sp.fy * ch, nx = X + cw / 2 + sp.nx * cw, ny = Y + ch / 2 + sp.ny * ch;
    x.save(); x.beginPath(); x.arc(cx, cy, rad + 4, 0, Math.PI * 2); x.fillStyle = '#fff'; x.fill(); x.beginPath(); x.arc(cx, cy, rad, 0, Math.PI * 2); x.clip();
    if (pics[e]) x.drawImage(pics[e], cx - rad, cy - rad, rad * 2, rad * 2); else { x.fillStyle = '#E4E7EE'; x.fillRect(cx - rad, cy - rad, rad * 2, rad * 2); x.fillStyle = '#6B6F86'; x.font = '600 24px Fraunces, Georgia, serif'; x.textAlign = 'center'; x.fillText(phInit(st[1]), cx, cy + 1); }
    x.restore();
    let fs2 = 30, nm = stDesk(st); x.textAlign = 'center'; x.font = '800 ' + fs2 + 'px Nunito, Arial, sans-serif'; while (x.measureText(nm).width > cw * .84 && fs2 > 15) { fs2--; x.font = '800 ' + fs2 + 'px Nunito, Arial, sans-serif'; } while (x.measureText(nm).width > cw * .84 && nm.length > 3) nm = nm.slice(0, -2) + '…';
    const tw = x.measureText(nm).width + 12; x.save(); x.translate(nx, ny); x.rotate((sp.nr || 0) * Math.PI / 180); x.fillStyle = '#F6E9D2'; x.strokeStyle = 'rgba(61,51,40,.55)'; x.lineWidth = 1.5; x.beginPath(); x.roundRect(-tw / 2, -fs2 * .7, tw, fs2 * 1.4, 6); x.fill(); x.stroke(); x.fillStyle = '#1B1F33'; x.fillText(nm, 0, 1); x.restore(); x.textAlign = 'left';
  });
  return cv.toDataURL('image/png');
}
ACT.stpng = async el => {
  el.disabled = true; let url; try { url = await stPng(); } catch (e) { el.disabled = false; return toast('The picture could not be made: ' + e.message); } el.disabled = false;
  const name = (S.st.cls || 'Class').replace(/[\\:*?"<>|]+/g, ' ').trim() + ' seating chart.png';
  try { const a = document.createElement('a'); a.href = url; a.download = name; document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove(); } catch (e) {}
  modal(`<h2>Seating Chart Picture</h2><img src="${url}" alt="Seating chart for ${esc(S.st.cls)}" style="width:100%;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:12px"><p class="small muted">It should be in your Downloads as “${esc(name)}”. If not, right-click the picture (or tap with two fingers on a Chromebook) and choose Save image as. It shows the room, names and photos only: no areas, pins or rules.</p><button class="btn wide" data-a="close">Done</button>`, true);
};
ACT.stfullx = () => { const d = $('#stfull'); if (d) d.remove(); try { if (document.fullscreenElement) document.exitFullscreen(); } catch (e) {} };
document.addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Escape' && $('#stfull')) ACT.stfullx(); });
/* ───────── group maker and name picker (teacher) ─────────
   Both use the class list and class photos from the seating chart. The group maker keeps apart the students marked Keep away from
   in that class's seating chart (other accommodations are not used). Absent students are left out. Only the teacher sees these pages. */
function gpLoad(state, view, paint) {
  call('api_seating', AUTH, state.cls).then(d => { state.data = d; state.cls = d.cls; if (!state.absent || state.absentFor !== d.cls) { state.absent = new Set(d.absent || []); state.absentFor = d.cls; } if (S.view === view) paint(); })
    .catch(e => { const b = $('#gpbody'); if (b) b.innerHTML = '<p>' + esc(e.message) + '</p>'; });
}
/* first names, with the rest only when two students in the class share a first name */
const gpName = (state, st) => { const f = stFirst(st[1]).toLowerCase(); return state.data.students.some(x => x[0] !== st[0] && stFirst(x[1]).toLowerCase() === f) ? st[1] : stFirst(st[1]); };
const gpFace = (st, cls) => `<span class="gpface${cls ? ' ' + cls : ''}">${st[2] ? `<img src="${esc(st[2])}" alt="">` : `<span>${esc(phInit(st[1]))}</span>`}</span>`;
const gpClasses = (state, act) => state.data.classes.length > 1 ? `<div class="seg stclasses" style="flex-wrap:wrap">${state.data.classes.map(x => `<button class="${x === state.cls ? 'on' : ''}" data-a="${act}" data-c="${esc(x)}">${esc(x)}<small>${(state.data.counts || {})[x] || 0} students</small></button>`).join('')}</div>` : '';
const gpAbsent = (state, act) => `<section class="card stack" style="gap:8px"><div class="row"><h3 class="grow">Who Is Here Today?</h3><span class="small muted">${state.data.absentNote ? esc(state.data.absentNote) + ' · ' : ''}${state.data.students.length - state.absent.size} here · highlighted names are here · tap a name to mark them absent</span></div>
  <div class="wrap">${state.data.students.map(s => `<button class="chip flat gpwho${state.absent.has(s[0]) ? ' out' : ' sel'}" data-a="${act}" data-e="${esc(s[0])}" aria-pressed="${!state.absent.has(s[0])}" aria-label="${esc(s[1])}, ${state.absent.has(s[0]) ? 'absent' : 'here'}">${esc(s[1])}</button>`).join('')}</div></section>`;
const gpHere = state => state.data.students.filter(s => !state.absent.has(s[0]));

/* groups: random, then swaps that never put more Keep away pairs together; the best of many tries wins */
/* mix: spread each level (Prep, Accel...) evenly: the students are dealt round the groups level by level, and the swaps that keep
   Keep away pairs apart only swap two students of the same level, so the mix stays even */
function gpMake(list, n, apart, mix) {
  const key = (a, b) => a < b ? a + '|' + b : b + '|' + a, bad = new Set(apart.map(r => key(r.a, r.b)));
  const deal = () => { if (!mix) return shuffle(list.slice()); const lv = [...new Set(list.map(s => s[3] || ''))]; return shuffle(lv).flatMap(l => shuffle(list.filter(s => (s[3] || '') === l))); };
  const cost = gs => { let c = 0; gs.forEach(g => { for (let i = 0; i < g.length; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < g.length; j++) if (bad.has(key(g[i][0], g[j][0]))) c++; }); return c; };
  let best = null;
  for (let t = 0; t < 60; t++) {
    const gs = Array.from({ length: n }, () => []), start = Math.floor(Math.random() * n); deal().forEach((s, i) => gs[(i + start) % n].push(s));
    let cur = cost(gs);
    for (let i = 0; i < 600 && cur > 0; i++) {
      const a = Math.floor(Math.random() * n), b = Math.floor(Math.random() * n); if (a === b || !gs[a].length || !gs[b].length) continue;
      const x = Math.floor(Math.random() * gs[a].length); let y = Math.floor(Math.random() * gs[b].length);
      if (mix && (gs[b][y][3] || '') !== (gs[a][x][3] || '')) { const same = gs[b].map((s, k) => k).filter(k => (gs[b][k][3] || '') === (gs[a][x][3] || '')); if (!same.length) continue; y = same[Math.floor(Math.random() * same.length)]; }
      const tmp = gs[a][x]; gs[a][x] = gs[b][y]; gs[b][y] = tmp;
      const nc = cost(gs); if (nc <= cur) cur = nc; else { gs[b][y] = gs[a][x]; gs[a][x] = tmp; }
    }
    if (!best || cur < best.cost) best = { cost: cur, gs: gs.map(g => g.slice()) };
    if (!cur) break;
  }
  const together = []; best.gs.forEach(g => { for (let i = 0; i < g.length; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < g.length; j++) if (bad.has(key(g[i][0], g[j][0]))) together.push([g[i], g[j]]); });
  return { groups: best.gs, together };
}
VIEW.groups = () => {
  S.gm = S.gm || { cls: '', how: 'count', n: 4, size: 3, groups: null };
  if (S.st && S.st.cls && S.gm.cls !== S.st.cls) { S.gm.cls = S.st.cls; S.gm.groups = null; }      /* the class open in the Seating chart, so its Keep away from pairs apply */
  gpLoad(S.gm, 'groups', gmPaint);
  return `<div class="stack"><div class="row"><div class="grow"><p class="eyebrow">Teacher</p><h1>Group Maker</h1></div></div>${teachNav('groups')}<div id="gpbody"><div class="splash"><div class="spin"></div></div></div></div>`;
};
function gmGroupsHtml(big) {
  const g = S.gm; if (!g.groups) return '';
  return `<div class="gmgrid${big ? ' big' : ''}">${g.groups.map((grp, i) => `<div class="gmcard" style="--gc:${ST_ZCOL[i % ST_ZCOL.length]}"><h3>Group ${i + 1}</h3><ul>${grp.map(s => `<li>${gpFace(s)}<b>${esc(gpName(g, s))}</b></li>`).join('')}</ul></div>`).join('')}</div>`;
}
function gmPaint() {
  const b = $('#gpbody'), g = S.gm; if (!b || !g.data) return;
  if (!g.data.students.length) { b.innerHTML = gpClasses(g, 'gmcls') + '<section class="card"><p>No students in this class yet.</p></section>'; return; }
  const here = gpHere(g).length, apart = ((g.data.chart || {}).rules || []).filter(r => r.t === 'apart');
  const stepper = (k, v, lo, hi) => `<span class="gmstep"><button class="chip flat" data-a="gmstep" data-k="${k}" data-d="-1" aria-label="Fewer">−</button><b class="num">${v}</b><button class="chip flat" data-a="gmstep" data-k="${k}" data-d="1" aria-label="More">+</button></span>`;
  const nOf = g.how === 'count' ? Math.min(g.n, Math.max(1, here)) : Math.max(1, Math.floor(here / g.size));
  b.innerHTML = `<div class="stack">${gpClasses(g, 'gmcls')}
    <section class="card stack" style="gap:12px">
      <div class="row" style="flex-wrap:wrap;gap:12px"><div class="seg">${[['count', 'Number of groups'], ['size', 'Students per group']].map(o => `<button class="${g.how === o[0] ? 'on' : ''}" data-a="gmhow" data-v="${o[0]}">${o[1]}</button>`).join('')}</div>
        ${g.how === 'count' ? `<span>Make ${stepper('n', g.n)} groups</span>` : `<span>Groups of ${stepper('size', g.size)}</span>`}
        <span class="small muted">${here} students here → ${nOf} ${nOf === 1 ? 'group' : 'groups'}</span></div>
      ${gmLevels(g).length > 1 ? `<label class="row small" style="gap:8px;align-items:center;font-weight:700"><input type="checkbox" id="gmmix"${g.mix !== false ? ' checked' : ''}> Mix ${esc(gmLevels(g).join(' and '))} students evenly across the groups</label>` : ''}
      <div class="row" style="flex-wrap:wrap"><button class="btn sun" data-a="gmmake">${g.groups ? 'Shuffle again' : 'Make groups'}</button>${g.groups ? '<button class="btn alt" data-a="gmfull">Projector view</button>' : ''}</div>
      <p class="small muted" style="margin:0">${apart.length ? `${apart.length} Keep away from ${apart.length === 1 ? 'pair is' : 'pairs are'} kept in different groups (from this class’s Seating chart).` : 'Tip: students marked Keep away from each other in the Seating chart are put in different groups.'} Other seating needs are not used here.</p>
      ${g.together && g.together.length ? `<p class="small" style="margin:0;color:#D64545">Could not keep apart: ${g.together.map(p => esc(gpName(g, p[0])) + ' and ' + esc(gpName(g, p[1]))).join('; ')}. Try fewer students per group or Shuffle again.</p>` : ''}
    </section>
    ${gmGroupsHtml(false)}
    ${gpAbsent(g, 'gmabs')}</div>`;
}
/* the levels among the students here (Prep, Accel...): Mix levels evenly shows only when there are two or more */
const gmLevels = g => [...new Set(gpHere(g).map(s => s[3]).filter(Boolean))];
document.addEventListener('change', e => { if (e.target && e.target.id === 'gmmix' && S.gm) S.gm.mix = e.target.checked; });
ACT.gmcls = el => { S.gm.cls = el.dataset.c; if (S.st) S.st.cls = el.dataset.c; S.gm.groups = null; S.gm.data = null; $('#gpbody').innerHTML = '<div class="splash"><div class="spin"></div></div>'; gpLoad(S.gm, 'groups', gmPaint); };
ACT.gmhow = el => { S.gm.how = el.dataset.v; gmPaint(); };
ACT.gmstep = el => { const k = el.dataset.k; S.gm[k] = Math.max(k === 'n' ? 1 : 2, Math.min(20, S.gm[k] + +el.dataset.d)); gmPaint(); };
ACT.gmabs = el => { const s = S.gm.absent, e = el.dataset.e; if (s.has(e)) s.delete(e); else s.add(e); if (S.gm.groups) S.gm.groups = S.gm.groups.map(gr => gr.filter(x => !s.has(x[0]))); gmPaint(); };
ACT.gmmake = () => {
  const g = S.gm, list = gpHere(g); if (!list.length) return toast('Everyone is marked absent.');
  const n = g.how === 'count' ? Math.min(g.n, list.length) : Math.max(1, Math.floor(list.length / g.size));
  const r = gpMake(list, n, ((g.data.chart || {}).rules || []).filter(x => x.t === 'apart'), g.mix !== false && gmLevels(g).length > 1); g.groups = r.groups; g.together = r.together; gmPaint();
};
ACT.gmfull = () => gpFull('Groups · ' + S.gm.cls, () => gmGroupsHtml(true), '<button class="btn sun" data-a="gmagain">Shuffle again</button>');
ACT.gmagain = () => { ACT.gmmake(); const d = $('#gpfull .gpfullbody'); if (d) d.innerHTML = gmGroupsHtml(true); };
/* projector view for both pages */

  document.body.appendChild(d);
  d.addEventListener('click', e => { const b = e.target.closest('[data-a]'); if (!b) return; e.stopPropagation(); if (ACT[b.dataset.a]) ACT[b.dataset.a](b); });
  try { const p = document.documentElement.requestFullscreen && document.documentElement.requestFullscreen(); if (p && p.catch) p.catch(() => {}); } catch (e) {}
}

VIEW.picker = () => {

const npUsed = () => { try { return new Set(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(npKey(S.np.cls)) || '[]')); } catch (e) { return new Set(); } };
const npSaveUsed = u => { try { localStorage.setItem(npKey(S.np.cls), JSON.stringify([...u])); } catch (e) {} };
/* one card on the reel: a square picture on the left and the name on the right (or just the name when pictures are off) */
/* a very long word gets a soft hyphen near its middle, so it can break onto two lines and stay big */
const npHy = n => n.split(' ').map(w => { if (w.length < 12) return w; let k = Math.ceil(w.length / 2); for (let d = 0; d < 3; d++) { if (/[aeiouy]/i.test(w[k - 1]) && !/[aeiouy]/i.test(w[k])) break; k++; } return w.slice(0, k) + '\u00AD' + w.slice(k); }).join(' ');
const npLen = n => Math.max(4, ...n.split(/[\s\u00AD]+/).map(x => x.length + 1), n.indexOf('\u00AD') < 0 ? n.length : 0);
function npCard(st) {
  const p = S.np, img = p.noPic ? '' : `<span class="npimg">${st[2] ? `<img src="${esc(st[2])}" alt="">` : esc(phInit(st[1]))}</span>`;
  return `<div class="npcard${p.noPic ? ' nopic' : ''}">${img}<b class="npname" style="--nl:${npLen(npHy(gpName(p, st)))}">${esc(npHy(gpName(p, st)))}</b></div>`;
}
/* a slot-machine reel: while spinning, a strip of names rolls up and slows down onto the chosen one */
function npStage(big) {
  const p = S.np, st = p.pick && p.data.students.find(s => s[0] === p.pick), find = e => p.data.students.find(s => s[0] === e);
  const inner = p.spinning && p.reel ? `<div class="npreel rolling"><div class="npstrip">${p.reel.map(e => npCard(find(e))).join('')}</div></div>`
    : st ? `<div class="npreel done"><div class="npstrip">${npCard(st)}</div></div>` : '<span class="npwait">Press Pick a name</span>';
  return `<div class="npstage${big ? ' big' : ''}" aria-live="polite">${inner}</div>`;
}
/* students skipped today (a secret: never picked, never shown in projector view); cleared the next day */
const npSkipKey = cls => 'sh-np-skip-' + cls;
/* when the server sends who is not here today (data.absent: the kiosk's check-ins), they are skipped too, unless the teacher moved them back (here) */
const npSkipRaw = () => { try { const o = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(npSkipKey(S.np.cls)) || '{}'); return o.d === S.today ? o : {}; } catch (e) { return {}; } };
const npSkip = () => { const o = npSkipRaw(), here = new Set(o.here || []), set = new Set(o.ids || []); ((S.np.data || {}).absent || []).forEach(e => { if (!here.has(e)) set.add(e); }); return set; };
const npSaveSkip = set => { const away = new Set((S.np.data || {}).absent || []), o = npSkipRaw(), here = new Set(o.here || []);
  away.forEach(e => { if (set.has(e)) here.delete(e); else here.add(e); });
  try { localStorage.setItem(npSkipKey(S.np.cls), JSON.stringify({ d: S.today, ids: [...set].filter(e => !away.has(e)), here: [...here] })); } catch (e) {} };
function npPaint() {
  const b = $('#gpbody'), p = S.np; if (!b || !p.data) return;
  if (!p.data.students.length) { b.innerHTML = gpClasses(p, 'npcls') + '<section class="card"><p>No students in this class yet.</p></section>'; return; }
  const used = npUsed(), skip = npSkip(), all = p.data.students;
  const still = all.filter(s => !skip.has(s[0]) && (p.repeat || !used.has(s[0]))), had = p.repeat ? [] : all.filter(s => used.has(s[0]) && !skip.has(s[0])), out = all.filter(s => skip.has(s[0]));
  const chips = list => list.length ? list.map(s => `<button class="chip flat" data-a="npwho" data-e="${esc(s[0])}">${esc(s[1])}</button>`).join('') : '<span class="small muted">Nobody</span>';
  b.innerHTML = `<div class="stack">${gpClasses(p, 'npcls')}
    <section class="card stack" style="gap:14px;align-items:center;text-align:center">${npStage(false)}
      <div class="row" style="flex-wrap:wrap;justify-content:center"><button class="btn sun npgo" data-a="npgo">Pick a name</button><button class="btn alt" data-a="npfull">Projector view</button></div>
      <label class="row" style="gap:8px;justify-content:center"><input type="checkbox" id="nprep" ${p.repeat ? '' : 'checked'} style="width:22px;height:22px"><span>No repeats until everyone has had a turn</span></label>
      <label class="row" style="gap:8px;justify-content:center"><input type="checkbox" id="nppic" ${p.noPic ? '' : 'checked'} style="width:22px;height:22px"><span>Show the student’s picture</span></label></section>
    <details class="card nplist"${p.open ? ' open' : ''}><summary><b>Picking list</b> <span class="small muted">only you see this · ${still.length} still to pick${p.repeat ? '' : ' · ' + had.length + ' had a turn'} · ${out.length} skipped today${p.data.absentNote ? ' · ' + esc(p.data.absentNote.toLowerCase()) : ''}</span></summary>
      <p class="small muted" style="margin:8px 0">Tap a name to move it. Skipped students are never picked; that list clears by itself tomorrow. Nothing here shows in projector view.</p>
      <div class="npcols"><div><h4>Still to Pick</h4><div class="wrap">${chips(still)}</div></div>
        ${p.repeat ? '' : `<div><h4>Had a Turn This Round</h4><div class="wrap">${chips(had)}</div>${had.length ? '<button class="btn alt sm" data-a="npreset" style="margin-top:8px">Start a new round</button>' : ''}</div>`}
        <div><h4>Skipped Today</h4><div class="wrap">${chips(out)}</div></div></div></details></div>`;
  const det = b.querySelector('.nplist'); if (det) det.addEventListener('toggle', () => { p.open = det.open; });
}
document.addEventListener('change', e => { if (e.target && e.target.id === 'nprep') { S.np.repeat = !e.target.checked; npPaint(); } if (e.target && e.target.id === 'nppic') { S.np.noPic = !e.target.checked; tmSave('sh-np-pic', S.np.noPic ? '' : '1'); npPaint(); } });
ACT.npcls = el => { S.np.cls = el.dataset.c; if (S.st) S.st.cls = el.dataset.c; S.np.pick = ''; S.np.data = null; $('#gpbody').innerHTML = '<div class="splash"><div class="spin"></div></div>'; gpLoad(S.np, 'picker', npPaint); };
ACT.npreset = () => { npSaveUsed(new Set()); npPaint(); toast('New round: everyone can be picked again.'); };
/* move one student between Still to pick, Had a turn and Skipped today */
ACT.npwho = el => { const e = el.dataset.e, st = S.np.data.students.find(s => s[0] === e); if (!st) return;
  const used = npUsed(), skip = npSkip(), now = skip.has(e) ? 'skip' : used.has(e) && !S.np.repeat ? 'had' : 'still', opt = (k, l) => `<button class="btn${now === k ? '' : ' alt'} wide" data-a="npmove" data-e="${esc(e)}" data-k="${k}">${l}</button>`;
  modal(`<h2>${esc(st[1])}</h2><div class="stack" style="gap:8px">${opt('still', 'Can be picked')}${S.np.repeat ? '' : opt('had', 'Had a turn this round')}${opt('skip', 'Skip today (secret)')}</div><button class="btn alt wide" data-a="close" style="margin-top:12px">Cancel</button>`); };
ACT.npmove = el => { const e = el.dataset.e, k = el.dataset.k, used = npUsed(), skip = npSkip();
  skip.delete(e); used.delete(e); if (k === 'skip') skip.add(e); if (k === 'had') used.add(e); npSaveSkip(skip); npSaveUsed(used); closeModal(); npPaint(); };
ACT.npgo = () => {
  const p = S.np; if (p.spinning) return; const skip = npSkip(), here = p.data.students.filter(s => !skip.has(s[0])); if (!here.length) return toast('Everyone is skipped today.');
  let used = npUsed(), pool = p.repeat ? here : here.filter(s => !used.has(s[0]));
  if (!pool.length) { used = new Set(); npSaveUsed(used); pool = here; toast('Everyone has had a turn. Starting a new round.'); }
  const winner = rand(pool), all = p.data.students, reel = []; for (let i = 0; i < 26; i++) reel.push(rand(all)[0]); reel.push(winner[0]);
  p.reel = reel; p.spinning = true; p.pick = '';
  document.querySelectorAll('.npstage').forEach(el => { el.outerHTML = npStage(el.classList.contains('big')); });
  const ms = 2600, finish = () => { if (!p.spinning) return; p.spinning = false; p.pick = winner[0]; if (!p.repeat) { used.add(winner[0]); npSaveUsed(used); } if (S.view === 'picker') npPaint(); document.querySelectorAll('#gpfull .npstage').forEach(el => { el.outerHTML = npStage(true); }); };
  document.querySelectorAll('.npreel.rolling .npstrip').forEach(strip => { const h = strip.firstElementChild ? strip.firstElementChild.offsetHeight : 0;
    if (strip.animate && h) strip.animate([{ transform: 'translateY(0)', easing: 'cubic-bezier(.2,.6,.35,1)' }, { transform: 'translateY(' + (-h * (reel.length - 1) - h * .18) + 'px)', offset: .9, easing: 'ease-in-out' }, { transform: 'translateY(' + (-h * (reel.length - 1)) + 'px)' }], { duration: ms, fill: 'forwards' }); });      /* rolls up, overshoots a little and settles, like a slot machine */
  setTimeout(finish, ms + 120);
};
ACT.npfull = () => gpFull('Name picker · ' + S.np.cls, () => npStage(true), '<button class="btn sun" data-a="npgo">Pick a name</button>');





/* ===== Hall Pass Kiosk adapter (after ASL Study's seating code) ===== */
ACT.kgo = el => { closeModal(); go(el.dataset.v); };
ACT.close = closeModal;

document.addEventListener('submit', e => { const f = e.target.closest('[data-f]'); if (!f) return; e.preventDefault(); if (FORM[f.dataset.f]) FORM[f.dataset.f](f); });
document.addEventListener('click', e => {
  if (e.target.id === 'modal') return closeModal();
  const el = e.target.closest('[data-a]'); if (!el || el.disabled) return;
  const fn = ACT[el.dataset.a]; if (fn) fn(el, e);
});
document.addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Escape' && !$('#modal').hidden) closeModal(); });

/* ----- Saved layouts: a room to reuse (desks only) or a whole arrangement (desks with students) ----- */
ACT.ksavelayout = () => {
  if (!S.st || !S.st.chart) return toast('Open a class first.');
  modal(`<form class="stack" data-f="ksavelayout">
    <h2 style="margin:0">Save as a layout</h2>
    <p class="muted" style="margin:0">Keep this room to use again for any class.</p>
    <label class="stack" style="gap:4px"><b>Name</b><input id="klname" maxlength="60" placeholder="Fall rows, Test day, Groups of 4…" required></label>
    <label class="row" style="gap:8px"><input type="radio" name="klwho" value="desks" checked> <span><b>Desks only</b> (a template: the room without students)</span></label>
    <label class="row" style="gap:8px"><input type="radio" name="klwho" value="students"> <span><b>Desks with students</b> (who sits where, pins and rules too)</span></label>
    <div class="row" style="gap:8px"><button class="btn sun" type="submit">Save layout</button><button class="btn ghost" type="button" data-a="close">Cancel</button></div>
  </form>`, true);
};
FORM.ksavelayout = f => {
  const name = $('#klname', f).value.trim(); if (!name) return;
  const withStudents = (f.querySelector('input[name=klwho]:checked') || {}).value === 'students';
  call('saveSeatingLayout', name, S.st.chart, withStudents)
    .then(() => { closeModal(); toast('Saved the layout “' + name + '”.'); })
    .catch(e => toast(e.message));
};
ACT.klayouts = () => {
  modal('<div class="splash" style="min-height:120px"><div class="spin"></div></div>', true);
  call('listSeatingLayouts').then(list => {
    S.kLayouts = list;
    const rows = list.length ? list.map((l, i) => `<div class="row" style="gap:8px;align-items:center;border-top:1px solid var(--line);padding:8px 0">
        <div class="grow"><b>${esc(l.name)}</b><div class="small muted">${l.withStudents ? 'Desks with students' : 'Desks only'} · ${l.desks} seats${l.updated ? ' · saved ' + esc(l.updated) : ''}</div></div>
        <button class="btn sun" data-a="kuselayout" data-i="${i}">Use for ${esc(S.st && S.st.cls || 'this class')}</button>
        <button class="btn ghost" data-a="kdellayout" data-i="${i}">Delete</button></div>`).join('')
      : '<p class="muted">No saved layouts yet. Build a room, then choose Save as a layout.</p>';
    modal(`<div class="stack"><h2 style="margin:0">Saved layouts</h2>${rows}<div><button class="btn ghost" data-a="close">Close</button></div></div>`, true);
  }).catch(e => { closeModal(); toast(e.message); });
};
ACT.kuselayout = el => {
  const l = (S.kLayouts || [])[+el.dataset.i]; if (!l || !S.st) return;
  modal(`<div class="stack"><h2 style="margin:0">Use “${esc(l.name)}”?</h2>
    <p>This replaces the seating chart for <b>${esc(S.st.cls)}</b>${l.withStudents ? '. Students who are not in this class are left off.' : '. Students can then be seated with Shuffle or by hand.'}</p>
    <div class="row" style="gap:8px"><button class="btn sun" data-a="kuselayoutgo" data-i="${el.dataset.i}">Use this layout</button><button class="btn ghost" data-a="close">Cancel</button></div></div>`, true);
};
ACT.kuselayoutgo = el => {
  const l = (S.kLayouts || [])[+el.dataset.i]; if (!l || !S.st) return;
  const chart = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(l.chart || {}));
  call('api_seatingSave', AUTH, S.st.cls, chart).then(() => { closeModal(); stLoad(S.st.cls); toast('Now using “' + l.name + '”.'); }).catch(e => toast(e.message));
};
ACT.kdellayout = el => {
  const l = (S.kLayouts || [])[+el.dataset.i]; if (!l) return;
  call('deleteSeatingLayout', l.name).then(() => ACT.klayouts()).catch(e => toast(e.message));
};

/* ----- Find my seat (?view=seat&cls=…&key=…): the class's chart with one student's desk lit up ----- */
function kioskFindSeat(cls, key) {
  call('api_seating', AUTH, cls).then(d => {
    const c = d.chart;
    if (!c || !Array.isArray(c.desks) || !c.desks.length) {
      view.innerHTML = '<div class="splash"><p class="muted">There is no seating chart for this class yet.</p></div>';
      return;
    }
    ['rules', 'zones'].forEach(k => { if (!Array.isArray(c[k])) c[k] = []; });
    c.seats = c.seats || {}; c.pins = c.pins || {}; c.items = c.items || {}; c.kinds = c.kinds || {};
    c.doors = c.doors || {}; c.face = c.face || {}; c.walls = c.walls || {}; if (!Array.isArray(c.off)) c.off = [];
    S.st = { cls: d.cls, mode: 'seat', pick: '', hl: key, data: d, chart: c };
    view.innerHTML = '<div id="stfull" class="seatfind">' + stGrid(true) + '</div>';
    const seat = $('.sts.hl'); if (seat && seat.scrollIntoView) seat.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center', inline: 'center' });
  }).catch(e => { view.innerHTML = '<div class="splash"><p class="muted">' + esc(e.message) + '</p></div>'; });
}

/* ----- Name picker: once a minute, fresh check-ins (a late arrival can be picked; someone who left for the day is skipped) ----- */
setInterval(() => {
  const p = S.np; if (S.view !== 'picker' || !p || !p.data || p.spinning || document.hidden) return;
  const cls = p.cls;
  call('api_seating', AUTH, cls, { absentOnly: true }).then(d => {
    if (S.view !== 'picker' || p.cls !== cls || p.spinning || !p.data) return;
    const before = JSON.stringify(p.data.absent || []);
    p.data.absent = d.absent || []; p.data.absentNote = d.absentNote || '';
    if (JSON.stringify(p.data.absent) !== before && $('#modal').hidden) npPaint();
  }).catch(() => {});
}, 60000);

/* ----- start ----- */
(function start() {
  const begin = p => {
    call('getSeatingLink').then(u => { kioskLink = u || ''; if (S.view === 'seats' && $('.knav')) go(S.view); }).catch(() => {});
    if (p.view === 'seat') {
      kioskFindSeat(p.cls || '', p.key || '');
      // No class named (the Projector's Seating chart box): the class in session, checked every 5 minutes
      if (!p.cls) setInterval(() => { if (!document.hidden) kioskFindSeat('', ''); }, 300000);
      return;
    }
    if (p.tool === 'picker' || p.tool === 'groups') {      /* a Tools window: just this tool, big */
      document.body.classList.add('ktools');
      go(p.tool);
      return;
    }
    S.st = { cls: p.cls || '', mode: 'seat', pick: '', tool: 'desk' };
    go('seats');
  };
  try { google.script.url.getLocation(loc => begin((loc && loc.parameter) || {})); }
  catch (e) { begin({}); }
})();

</script>
</body>
</html>
